<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063022' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49063022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/113236-us-prosecutors-charge-atlanta-man-after-grapheneos-phone.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US citizen charged after GrapheneOS phone wipes during airport search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国公民在机场安检时因 GrapheneOS 自动清除数据而被起诉</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1050</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49063022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The case highlights how privacy-focused devices like GrapheneOS can trigger data wipe during border checks, leading to legal action. It underscores tensions between personal data protection, law enforcement, and the risk of unintended data loss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一起案例显示隐私导向设备在机场安检时可能因安全特性触发数据擦除，进而引发起诉。凸显了个人数据保护、执法权力与意外数据丢失之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techspot.com/images2/news/bigimage/2026/07/2026-07-26-image-4.jpg' data-full='https://www.techspot.com/images2/news/bigimage/2026/07/2026-07-26-image-4.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49113929' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49113929' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uefa.com/news-media/news/02a7-213a92896eb0-54dfbf454e3b-1000--statement-on-behalf-of-uefa-and-its-55-national-associations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UEFA and its national associations will not participate in FIFA competitions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧足联及55个成员协会宣布退出FIFA赛事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 665</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113929'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UEFA and 55 national associations announced they will not participate in FIFA competitions, signaling a governance dispute between regional bodies and FIFA. The move could disrupt the international calendar and pressure FIFA to concede on reform terms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲足联与55个成员协会宣布不参与FIFA赛事，标志着与FIFA在治理与日程安排上的公开分歧。此举可能扰乱国际日历，促使对改革条款的谈判加剧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://editorial.uefa.com/resources/02a7-213acb6c89f6-6c8e1ae54e77-1000/uefa_corporate_logo_all_crops.png' data-full='https://editorial.uefa.com/resources/02a7-213acb6c89f6-6c8e1ae54e77-1000/uefa_corporate_logo_all_crops.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49076057' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49076057' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/position-open-weights-models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our position on open-weights models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于开放权重模型的立场</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1713</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49076057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic outlines its stance on open-weights models, emphasizing safety, governance, and responsible sharing over unrestricted release. It discusses the risk of misuse and proposes criteria for when and how weights should be shared or accessed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 阐述对开放权重模型的立场，强调在安全、治理与负责任共享之间取得平衡，而非盲目公开。文中指出若在缺乏防护的情况下公开权重可能被滥用，并提出负责任共享与准入控制的标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/62993bb857986e2808c2a75024f384e7b85312b6-2400x1254.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/62993bb857986e2808c2a75024f384e7b85312b6-2400x1254.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063754' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49063754' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PGSimCity - How PostgreSQL Works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PGSimCity：PostgreSQL 的工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 912</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49063754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PGSimCity uses a city-building metaphor to illuminate PostgreSQL internals, from storage and indexing to the query planner and executor. It helps readers understand how MVCC, WAL, and background processes combine to make PostgreSQL reliable and scalable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PGSimCity 以城市场景比喻讲解 PostgreSQL 的内部结构，包括存储、索引、查询优化器和执行器。帮助读者理解 MVCC、WAL 等机制是如何协同工作，提升可靠性与可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' data-full='https://nikolays.github.io/PGSimCity/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49098510' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49098510' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/drumih/turbo-fieldfare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Open-source engine running Gemma 4 26B in 2 GB RAM on any M-series Mac</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在任意 M-series Mac 的 2GB RAM 上运行 Gemma 4 26B 的开源引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 896</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 330</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098510'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open-source engine demonstrates running Gemma 4 26B with only 2 GB RAM on M-series Macs, using efficient quantization and memory management. This lowers the barrier to local LLM deployment and shows consumer hardware can still handle mid-size models with clever optimizations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个开源引擎在 M-series Mac 上仅用 2GB RAM 就能运行 Gemma 4 26B，依靠高效量化与内存管理。此举降低本地部署中等规模模型的门槛，显示消费级硬件通过优化仍具备可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49102774' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://christianselig.com/2026/07/vision-pro-house/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The coolest use for the Vision Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最酷的 Vision Pro 用法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49102774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 823</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49102774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49102774'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on look at turning Vision Pro into a home-control hub, overlaying dashboards and controls into your living space. The piece illustrates how AR can blend physical devices with digital control, hinting at future everyday use of consumer XR hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实际演示，揭示将 Vision Pro 打造成家庭控制中心的可能性——在现实空间中覆盖仪表板和设备控制。该探讨展示了增强现实如何把物理设备与数字控制融合在一起，预示着普通消费者对 XR 硬件的日常使用前景。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49112744' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49112744' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/07/read-this-before-you-buy-that-tv-streaming-stick/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Read this before you buy that TV streaming stick</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>买电视流媒体棒前该知道的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 799</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112744' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112744'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Smart TV sticks often ship with limited security and privacy protections, making them an easy attack vector for malware and data leakage. When shopping, prioritize reputable brands, timely firmware updates, and configurable privacy settings to minimize risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电视流媒体棒常常安全与隐私保护不足，容易成为恶意软件与数据泄露的入口。选购时应关注品牌信誉、固件更新及时性以及可配置的隐私设置，以降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/h96-amazon.png' data-full='https://krebsonsecurity.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/h96-amazon.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49080664' data-tags='Science,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49080664' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.data.jma.go.jp/multi/quake/quake_detail.html?eventID=20260728163528&amp;lang=en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>7.1 Earthquake in Japan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本发生7.1级地震</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49080664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 795</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49080664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49080664'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An about 7.1-magnitude earthquake struck Japan, triggering emergency alerts and shaking across affected regions. Authorities are assessing damage, power outages, and aftershock risks, highlighting resilience needs for infrastructure and critical facilities in disaster response planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本发生7.1级地震，政府发出紧急警报，震感覆盖多个区域。官方正在评估损毁、断电和余震风险，基础设施和关键设施的韧性成为灾后评估的核心议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.data.jma.go.jp/multi/images/common/logo.gif' data-full='https://www.data.jma.go.jp/multi/images/common/logo.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.superlogical.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Superlogical</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超逻辑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 776</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 452</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098965'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new project named Superlogical surfaces, signaling a push into advanced tooling around logical reasoning and automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个名为 Superlogical 的新项目浮出水面，标志着在逻辑推理与自动化工具领域的推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.superlogical.com/og.webp' data-full='https://www.superlogical.com/og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49068738' data-tags='AI,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49068738' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/HedgieMarkets/status/2081534588485296565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI companies are shredding rare books</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI公司正在撕碎珍稀书籍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 771</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 491</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49068738'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports claim AI companies are shredding rare books to feed training data, raising alarms about data sourcing and cultural preservation. The trend highlights the tension between rapid AI development and safeguarding irreplaceable artifacts, prompting calls for governance and licensing reforms. Experts warn the losses could be irreversible if these materials are discarded before proper archival decisions are made.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道称AI公司在收集训练数据时撕碎珍稀书籍，这引发对数据来源与文化保护的担忧。此趋势突显在推动AI高速发展时保护不可替代文献的难题，促使推动治理与许可框架的改革。若属实，损失可能是不可逆的，相关机构被敦促做出更审慎的档案保存决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HOMX3t1XYAA3Pr7.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HOMX3t1XYAA3Pr7.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49112232' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49112232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/changelog/2026-07-30-stacked-pull-requests-are-now-in-public-preview/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stacked PRs are now live on GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 现已上线堆叠 PR（Stacked PRs）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 767</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stacked PRs enable multiple dependent pull requests to be submitted and reviewed together, currently in public preview. This workflow can streamline complex changes, reduce review churn, and improve collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>堆叠 PR 允许将相互依赖的多个合并请求打包提交与评审，现处于公开预览阶段。该工作流有助于简化复杂改动、降低评审成本、提升协作效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Changelog-Stacks-NewRelease-Unfurl.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Changelog-Stacks-NewRelease-Unfurl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49118781' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49118781' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://earendil.com/posts/session-portability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The session you cannot take with you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你带不走的会话</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 751</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49118781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Session portability is a growing problem as users expect to pick up where they left off across devices. The piece surveys approaches like portable session tokens, device linking, and cryptographic handoffs, weighing security and UX trade-offs. The takeaway is that truly portable sessions could unlock seamless multi-device experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着用户期望在多设备间无缝继续工作，会话可移植性成为一个日益突出的问题。文中探讨了可移植会话令牌、设备绑定与密钥交接等方案，并权衡安全性与用户体验的取舍。真正的会话可移植性或将解锁无缝的多设备使用体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/session-portability.png' data-full='https://earendil.com/static/og/posts/session-portability.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49095865' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49095865' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://koreader.rocks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KOReader</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KOReader：跨平台的开源电子书阅读器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49095865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 720</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49095865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49095865'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>KOReader remains a robust, open-source e-reader that supports a wide range of formats and platforms, including e-ink devices. It emphasizes customization, annotations, and privacy-friendly features not always available in closed apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KOReader 依然是一款强大的跨平台开源电子书阅读器，支持多种格式与设备，包括电子墨水设备。它强调可定制、注释功能以及在封闭应用中不易实现的隐私友好特性。文章凸显了KOReader如何继续赋能读者，让用户对设备拥有更多控制权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://koreader.rocks/koreader-logo.png' data-full='https://koreader.rocks/koreader-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49119559' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49119559' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/updates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek-V4-Flash Update</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek-V4-Flash 更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 708</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49119559'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek releases a firmware update for the V4 Flash platform, focusing on stability, performance, and new features for data indexing and retrieval. The update underscores ongoing optimization of flash-based search systems and developer tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 为 V4 Flash 平台推出固件更新，聚焦稳定性、性能，并带来对数据索引与检索的新功能。此次更新凸显对基于闪存的搜索系统及开发工具的持续优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' data-full='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49084938' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://citizens-initiative.europa.eu/initiatives/details/2026/000011_en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stop Killing the Internet: No Digital ID and No Age Verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>停止肆意‘杀死互联网’：拒绝数字身份与年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 701</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49084938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A campaign arguing against digital IDs and age verification for online services, advocating privacy-preserving options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项倡议反对在线服务中的数字身份和年龄验证，主张隐私友好、可替代的保护方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://citizens-initiative.europa.eu/profiles/contrib/ewcms/modules/ewcms_seo/assets/images/eu-socialmedia-fallback.png' data-full='https://citizens-initiative.europa.eu/profiles/contrib/ewcms/modules/ewcms_seo/assets/images/eu-socialmedia-fallback.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49126604' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49126604' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/yc-software/qm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>qm – Multiplayer agent harness for work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>qm：面向工作场景的多人代理框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49126604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49126604'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively. It provides orchestration, tasking, and visibility across agent-driven workflows, helping teams tackle complex problems more efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。它提供编排、任务分发和对代理驱动工作流的可视化，帮助团队更高效地解决复杂问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6829f6872e88bca87e7d85c7d6497b3246daac80d02fc34591f8b1e055875e33/yc-software/qm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6829f6872e88bca87e7d85c7d6497b3246daac80d02fc34591f8b1e055875e33/yc-software/qm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49083314' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49083314' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lji.org/news-events/news/post/new-hiv-vaccine-shows-unprecedented-success-in-preclinical-study/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New HIV vaccine shows unprecedented success in preclinical study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型HIV疫苗在前临床研究中取得前所未有的成功</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49083314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 654</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49083314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49083314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new HIV vaccine candidate shows unprecedented efficacy in preclinical studies, signaling potential breakthrough. However, preclinical success does not guarantee human efficacy; several hurdles remain. If replicated in early human trials, it could reshape HIV vaccine development and open new avenues for immunogen design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款新型HIV疫苗候选在前临床研究中展现前所未有的有效性，给HIV疫苗研发带来希望。需要注意的是，前临床结果并不保证在人类身上也有效，仍有多项挑战待解。若在早期人体试验中得到证实，这一突破可能推动疫苗设计的进一步突破，并改变相关研究方向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49086788' data-tags='Web,Career,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49086788' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elizabethtai.com/2026/06/10/substack-writers-you-need-a-website/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Substack writers, you need a website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Substack 作家，你需要拥有一个个人网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49086788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 637</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49086788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 344</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49086788'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relying on a platform like Substack can be limiting; owning a personal website gives creators control, SEO, and diversification of revenue streams. The note encourages Substack writers to steward their own online presence with simple, durable setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>仅依赖 Substack 容易受制于平台变动，拥有个人网站能为创作者带来更多控制权、搜索流量和收入来源。建议 Substack 作者建立简洁、可持续的自有站点来提升长期影响力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/elizabethtai.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/pexels-photo-16622632.jpeg?fit=1200%2C795&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/elizabethtai.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/pexels-photo-16622632.jpeg?fit=1200%2C795&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49111237' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49111237' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/blog/gemini-robotics-2-brings-whole-body-intelligence-to-robots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini Robotics 2 brings whole body intelligence to robots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini Robotics 2 将整身智能引入机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49111237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49111237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 525</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49111237'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini Robotics 2 expands whole-body intelligence for robots, coordinating sensing, planning, and actuation across the entire body. It leverages large-language-model-driven planning to orchestrate multi-limb manipulation and locomotion, enabling more flexible, goal-driven behavior in unstructured environments. This marks a step toward robots that can reason about tasks at a higher level rather than relying on hand-built controllers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini Robotics 2 扩展了机器人整身智能，协同身体各部分进行感知、规划和执行。它利用基于大模型的规划来统一多肢的动作与移动，使机器人在非结构化环境中更具灵活性与目标导向。标志着让机器人以更高层次的任务推理来完成工作的一步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/VZ5KwQMxv9xBcQnYipsQB2EUj3oX1yvFYLktIamY8V2a76Y6ctEEuaLF59TuPdnaVn6OAMINDilqnuhju1O-AXc7QlOVmcogjskrWxS7xVQ1mc5S7g=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/VZ5KwQMxv9xBcQnYipsQB2EUj3oX1yvFYLktIamY8V2a76Y6ctEEuaLF59TuPdnaVn6OAMINDilqnuhju1O-AXc7QlOVmcogjskrWxS7xVQ1mc5S7g=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49127306' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49127306' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/hugging-face-intrusion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailscale didn&#x27;t stop the Hugging Face intrusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailscale 未阻止 Hugging Face 入侵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49127306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49127306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face disclosed a security incident in which attackers bypassed protections despite Tailscale, underscoring that zero-trust networking is not a sole defense. The post details how leaked credentials enabled access and the lessons about defense-in-depth and secret hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face 公开了一起安全事件，攻击者在使用 Tailscale 的情况下仍然进入内部系统，说明零信任网络并非万用钥匙。文中描述了凭证泄露如何促成入侵，并强调需要多层防护与良好密钥管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=0dafcba5-55df-4373-be5e-564b368a27af' data-full='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=0dafcba5-55df-4373-be5e-564b368a27af' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49103285' data-tags='AI,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49103285' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/ai-s-top-startups-are-barely-publishing-their-research' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI&#x27;s top startups are barely publishing their research</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的顶尖初创几乎不公开研究成果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 604</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 315</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49103285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Top AI startups publish little peer-reviewed research, preferring blog posts and press releases. This sparsity raises concerns about reproducibility, safety, and how quick advances are validated, as powerful models race ahead.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一些领先的 AI 初创公司公开的同行评审研究很少，更多依赖博客、公告。研究不透明可能影响结果复现与安全评估，也让快速迭代中的能力提升难以被独立验证。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49112867' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49112867' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/advancing-the-price-performance-frontier-with-gpt-5-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Advancing the price-performance frontier with GPT‑5.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以 GPT‑5.6 提升性价比前沿</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49112867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 394</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49112867'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI outlines how GPT-5.6 boosts price-performance through efficiency improvements and lower per-token costs. The upgrade could enable cheaper, faster AI inference for developers and businesses, widening access to advanced models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 介绍了 GPT‑5.6 在效率与成本方面的提升，降低每令牌成本，提升性价比。这一升级有望让开发者和企业以更低成本获得更快的推理能力，进一步普及高阶模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3YlohIkWAmTKqtreNc2Qwt/1ba93da6a8cb574aa0b299277874dbac/Frame__3_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3YlohIkWAmTKqtreNc2Qwt/1ba93da6a8cb574aa0b299277874dbac/Frame__3_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49089755' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49089755' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex-security' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex Security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 591</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49089755'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI released Codex Security as an open-source project, inviting security researchers to inspect and improve the security of Codex-powered tooling. The project provides tooling for auditing prompts, monitoring outputs, and identifying vulnerabilities across code-generation pipelines. By open-sourcing it, OpenAI accelerates community-driven security research and invites broader scrutiny of AI-enabled coding systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Codex Security 开源，面向安全研究者提供对 Codex 相关工具的审计与改进能力。该项目支持对 prompts、输出和代码生成流程中的潜在漏洞进行分析，并提升对 AI 辅助编码系统的可审计性。此举可能加速社区在 AI 安全领域的研究与防护工具普及。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4f6065cf6e0c5afd7891abd9f6003e14e6ebf5695f62bda7c552726a77337532/openai/codex-security' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4f6065cf6e0c5afd7891abd9f6003e14e6ebf5695f62bda7c552726a77337532/openai/codex-security' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49136821' data-tags='Web,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49136821' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openrss.org/blog/how-google-helped-destroy-adoption-of-rss-feeds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Google helped destroy adoption of RSS feeds (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌如何助推RSS订阅的衰落（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49136821'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google’s product and policy choices helped marginalize RSS, accelerating its decline in adoption. Centralized platforms controlled feed discovery and access, leaving RSS ecosystems with limited visibility; for builders, the takeaway is to rely on open standards and independent readers rather than platform-dictated discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾谷歌的一系列产品与策略如何弱化RSS的普及，推动其走向边缘。平台对内容发现的垄断让RSS缺乏可见度，开发者应坚持开放标准与独立阅读器，以避免被平台支配的发现机制所束缚。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://openrss.orghttps://openrss.org/media/google-links-break.jpg' data-full='https://openrss.orghttps://openrss.org/media/google-links-break.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 563</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49120299'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731 is assessed across intelligence, performance, and price, highlighting where it excels and where costs rise. The analysis helps buyers weigh value when choosing between models for inference workloads and edge deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731在智能能力、性能和性价比方面进行了评测，点出其强项与成本权衡。分析为在推理任务及边缘部署中选型提供价值判断。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49120097' data-tags='AI,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49120097' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/security/chrome-stronger-with-every-update/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google fixed more Chrome bugs in June than over the past two years, thanks to AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌靠 AI，在六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年总和</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 564</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49120097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google credits AI-assisted triage and patch generation for fixing more Chrome bugs in June than in the previous two years combined. This shows how AI tooling can accelerate vulnerability response and elevate browser hardening. It could foreshadow broader shifts in security workflows across software teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌称借助 AI 的分流与补丁生成，六月修复的 Chrome 漏洞数量超过过去两年的总和。这显示出 AI 工具能加速漏洞响应、提升浏览器安全性。此举或预示软件团队在安全工作流程上的更广泛变革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Screenshot_2026-07-17_at_5.00.43PM.width-1300.png' data-full='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Screenshot_2026-07-17_at_5.00.43PM.width-1300.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kimi.com/code/docs/en/kimi-code/models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi K3-256k</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi K3-256k</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49101852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 487</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49101852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49101852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi unveils K3-256k, a model with extended context length that can handle longer code and reasoning sessions. The move signals a push toward more capable AI coding assistants and longer, more coherent interactions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi 发布 K3-256k 模型，具备更长的上下文容量，适合处理更长的代码和推理对话。这反映出在代码助手领域追求更强大的对话连贯性和可处理任务的趋势。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49138188'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diátaxis provides four content types to cover different reader needs: tutorials, how-tos, references, and explanations. By aligning information to these categories, teams can craft documentation that supports both learning and practical use, improving overall clarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Diátaxis 将文档分成教程、使用方法、参考手册和讲解四类，以满足不同读者的需求。按这四种用途组织内容，有助于新手学习、老手快速查阅，并提升整份文档的一致性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lockwood.dev/ai/2026/07/27/how-is-the-bun-rewrite-in-rust-going.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How is the Bun rewrite in Rust going?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bun 的 Rust 重写进展如何？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49067854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49067854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 375</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49067854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Progress on Bun&#x27;s Rust rewrite is moving forward with early benchmarks hinting at faster startup and improved safety. The update discusses remaining compatibility gaps with Node.js tooling, migration challenges for developers, and how the rewrite could affect Bun&#x27;s ecosystem and performance relative to its Zig-based roots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun 的 Rust 重写项目正在推进，初步基准显示启动更快、内存安全性更高。重写仍面临与 Node.js 工具链的兼容性差距、开发者迁移成本等挑战，同时需要评估其对生态与性能相对于现有 Zig 版本的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：一个可放在房间里的物理上逼真的黑洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 464</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49021270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This project presents a compact, physically plausible black hole model you can own, designed to help people grasp gravity and spacetime concepts. It spotlights how educational demos can translate complex astrophysics into tangible intuition for learners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目提供一个可放在房间里的物理黑洞模型，帮助直观理解引力与时空概念。它展示了如何把复杂的天体物理知识转化为可触及的教育演示，提升公众理解。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49057241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Htmx 4.0 is released exclusively on Game Boy, a playful demonstration of bringing modern lightweight web UI concepts to retro hardware. The stunt sparks ideas about portability, WASM, and extreme constraint-driven design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Htmx 4.0 在 Game Boy 上独家发布，展示了将现代轻量级网页 UI 概念带到复古硬件的趣味尝试，引发对可移植性、WASM 及极端约束设计的思考。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A curated roundup of notable advances across mathematics and theoretical computer science, illustrating how new proofs, models, and algorithms broaden what we can prove and compute. The insights span core areas like complexity theory, probabilistic methods, and mathematical foundations that influence cryptography, optimization, and AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一组精选，聚焦数学与理论计算机科学领域的重大突破，展示新的证明、模型和算法如何扩展我们能证明和计算的边界。相关进展跨越复杂性理论、概率方法等领域，对密码学、优化和人工智能的基础研究具有深远影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49109440' data-tags='Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49109440' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://remysharp.com/links/2026-07-23-35890312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;VPNs are lawful technical tools,&#x27; says EU Court in landmark copyright ruling</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟法院：VPN是合法的技术工具——具有里程碑意义的版权裁决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 433</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49109440'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU Court ruled that VPNs are lawful technical tools that enable legitimate uses like privacy and cross-border information access. It draws a clear boundary against treating ordinary VPN use as infringing, which could influence enforcement strategies and platform liability going forward. For tech users, it reinforces VPNs as a standard part of digital privacy and access strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟法院裁定VPN属于合法的技术工具，支持隐私保护和跨境信息获取等正当用途。裁决明确反对将普通VPN使用定为侵权行为，可能影响日后的执法策略和平台责任。对技术用户而言，VPN成为数字隐私与接入策略的常规工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://remysharp.com/images/avatar-2022-square.avif' data-full='https://remysharp.com/images/avatar-2022-square.avif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 430</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A forensic-style timeline details how a Frontier Lab agent intrusion unfolded in July 2026, covering initial access, privilege escalation, and data exfiltration steps. The post pulls out defensive lessons for securing AI agent ecosystems and interdisciplinary attack paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以时间线方式梳理 Frontier Lab 的代理入侵事件（2026年7月），从初始入侵到权限提升再到数据外泄逐步揭示。文中强调对 AI 代理生态系统的安全防护和跨领域攻击路径的应对要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49076923' data-tags='Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49076923' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nypost.com/2026/07/26/us-news/netflix-exec-goes-ballistic-after-being-fired-for-stunning-trust-exercise-confession-at-retreat-suit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Netflix employee fired for sharing personal details in retreat trust exercise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Netflix 员工因在 retreat 信任活动中分享个人信息被解雇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076923' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 488</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49076923'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Netflix employee was fired after sharing personal details during a retreat trust exercise, raising questions about privacy, corporate culture, and what kinds of disclosures are acceptable in team-building settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名Netflix员工在团建信任活动中分享个人信息而被解雇，引发关于隐私、企业文化以及此类团建活动中可接受披露内容的讨论。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 426</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49036068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供处理沸水风险的实用指南，包括烫伤急救、预防要点和厨房/户外的安全做法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49093434' data-tags='Design,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49093434' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.datagubbe.se/scenegui/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>User Interfaces of the Demo Scene</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Demo Scene 的用户界面设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 423</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49093434'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The demo scene has long been about pushing visuals and sound, but its UI choices deserve attention too. This overview surveys the user interfaces used in demo productions, from HUDs to control schemes, showing how readability and timing shape the audience experience. It also touches on tooling constraints and cross-platform considerations that shape these interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示场景长期强调视觉与声音的冲击，其用户界面设计同样值得关注。文章梳理了演示作品中的各种 UI 形式，从 HUD 到控制方案，展示了可读性与节奏如何影响观众体验。还讨论了工具限制与跨平台因素对 UI 的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.datagubbe.se/scenegui/p/elitesinuesproducer-menu.png' data-full='https://www.datagubbe.se/scenegui/p/elitesinuesproducer-menu.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49090607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 417</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49090607' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 118</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49090607'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN post about a userscript that reduces tab clutter by changing how Hacker News links open. It saves time for power users by eliminating duplicated tabs and streamlining navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN：为避免每个 Hacker News 链接都要打开两个标签页，我写了一个用户脚本。这个小改动将链接在一个标签页中打开，减少标签页混乱，为经常浏览 HN 的用户节省时间，提升日常工作流效率。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Seedance 2.5</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49138302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 417</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49138302' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49138302'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seedance 2.5 updates a content creation workflow with flexible referencing and one-take creation. It enables creators to fluidly link assets and references, reducing friction in long-form production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Seedance 2.5 为内容创作工作流带来灵活的引用和一镜到底式的创作能力，使创作者能够更自然地在作品中嵌入可重用的素材与引用，降低制作摩擦。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49055169' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49055169' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/40700-grapheneos-protections-against-data-extraction-from-locked-devices' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS protections against data extraction from locked devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 对锁定设备数据提取的保护机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49055169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49055169' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49055169'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS explains how its security model minimizes data leakage from locked devices, using robust encryption, strict isolation, and anti-forensic measures. The post clarifies what data remains inaccessible when the device is locked and what this means for user privacy and enterprise security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 解释了在设备锁定时如何降低数据被提取的风险，依托强加密、严格隔离和防取证机制。文中说明锁屏状态下哪些数据不可访问，以及这对用户隐私和企业安全意味着什么。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' data-full='https://discuss.grapheneos.org/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49121980' data-tags='Business,Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49121980' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hughhowey.com/the-end-of-an-era/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The End of an Era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个时代的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49121980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49121980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 434</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49121980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Howey’s piece reflects on the end of an era in publishing, likely driven by AI, digital distribution, and changing reader behavior. It argues the industry is transitioning to a new era where authors retain more control and new business models emerge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Howey 的文章声称一个时代在出版界落幕，受AI、数字发行与读者行为变化推动。作者认为行业正在向一个新纪元转变，作者掌控力增强，新商业模式正在形成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hughhowey.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Screenshot-2026-07-31-at-6.22.53-AM-1024x516.png' data-full='https://hughhowey.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Screenshot-2026-07-31-at-6.22.53-AM-1024x516.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49107950' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49107950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://android-developers.googleblog.com/2026/07/google-play-age-signals-api-safer-experiences.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google will expand age checks on Android worldwide till the end of the year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌将把 Android 的年龄验证扩展至全球，直至今年年底</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49107950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 407</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49107950' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 496</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49107950'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is expanding age checks across Android worldwide, leveraging the Play Age Signals API to better enforce age-appropriate experiences. The expansion will affect developers who must align apps with stricter verification and data practices, while users gain improved protections for younger audiences. This move highlights ongoing privacy-preserving efforts in app ecosystems and the tension between safety and friction for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌宣布将 Android 的年龄验证扩展至全球，直至今年年底完成，借助 Age Signals API 提升对未成年用户的保护并提供更合适的应用体验。开发者需要适应更严格的身份验证和数据处理要求，用户隐私也需要在合规框架下得到保障。此举凸显了应用生态系统中对安全与用户体验之间平衡的持续关注。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49093569' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49093569' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/jailbroken-kindle-proxy-tun-modes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>More Tailscale tricks for your jailbroken Kindle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为越狱 Kindle 的更多 Tailscale 技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 402</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49093569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stretching VPNs to unusual hardware tests the limits of enterprise-grade tooling. The post shares practical Tailscale tricks for a jailbroken Kindle, showing how proxy and TUN modes can route the device into private networks. It underscores the versatility of VPN overlays for edge devices and tinkering with embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 VPN 延展到罕见的边缘设备上测试工具链的极限。本文分享在越狱 Kindle 上使用 Tailscale 的实用技巧，演示代理与 TUN 模式如何将设备接入私有网络。此举凸显了 VPN 覆盖在嵌入式设备上的灵活性，也鼓励对边缘设备的网络探索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=42fbec2c-0569-42cb-86c8-dbf1e2f2fa4a' data-full='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=42fbec2c-0569-42cb-86c8-dbf1e2f2fa4a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
