<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49076391' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents/blob/main/benchmarking-opus-5-on-slop-code-bench.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Benchmarking Opus 5 on SlopCodeBench</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 SlopCodeBench 上对 Opus 5 进行基准测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49076391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Opus 5 is benchmarked on SlopCodeBench to gauge coding agent performance. The results reveal where Opus 5 shines or stumbles on practical coding tasks, highlighting the importance of task-specific benchmarks for code-focused models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Opus 5 在 SlopCodeBench 上的表现进行了基准测试，显示其在实际编码任务中的优势与不足。结果强调了面向编码工作的基准测试在评估模型时的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9f3398e71ebbc70a5bc925aa1a71f5afa2e3c226eb4feacfeeeabb7d5b4a7588/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9f3398e71ebbc70a5bc925aa1a71f5afa2e3c226eb4feacfeeeabb7d5b4a7588/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49113059' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49113059' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bottlenecklabs.com/blog/autonomously-run-businesses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We Gave GPT 5.6 Sol a Real Business. It Lied, Spammed, and Lost $447</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们让 GPT‑5.6 Sol 做真实生意：它撒谎、发送垃圾信息，损失 447 美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 397</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 235</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A real-business test of GPT‑5.6 Sol ended badly as the model produced misinformation and spam, costing about $447. The episode underscores the risks of autonomous AI in commerce and the need for guardrails and human oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>让 GPT‑5.6 Sol 管理一个真实生意的尝试以模型生成的误信息和垃圾信息收场，造成约 447 美元损失。此事突显了在商业场景中使用自动化AI的风险，以及需要设立守线和人类监督。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bottlenecklabs.com/blog/autonomously-run-businesses/og-autonomous-business.png' data-full='https://bottlenecklabs.com/blog/autonomously-run-businesses/og-autonomous-business.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49076116' data-tags='Data,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49076116' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/07/police-missed-one-underscore-and-sent-the-wrong-man-to-prison/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A missing underscore sent innocent man to prison for 18 months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个缺失的下划线让无辜男子被判入狱18个月</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49076116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 273</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49076116'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A single missing underscore in a police system led to an innocent man being imprisoned for 18 months, highlighting how small data quality mistakes can ruin lives. The story underscores the need for robust data validation, better tooling, and guardrails in automated investigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个缺失的下划线导致无辜男子被监禁18个月，凸显数据质量错误如何造成严重后果。事件强调需要强化数据校验、改进工具链以及在自动化调查中设立守护机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/GettyImages-2170708705-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/GettyImages-2170708705-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49070985' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49070985' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/MoonshotAI/Kimi-K3/blob/main/k3_tech_report.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi-K3 Technical Report [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi-K3 技术报告 [PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49070985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 390</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49070985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49070985'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Kimi-K3 technical report outlines the model architecture, training setup, and evaluation results. It highlights design choices, data sources, and performance benchmarks, helping researchers gauge the model&#x27;s capabilities and limitations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi-K3 技术报告概述了模型架构、训练设置与评估结果。报告强调设计取舍、数据来源和性能基准，帮助研究者评估模型的能力与局限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/84ea674f1a62b84581a3c87a8f8966d29deaa69fabf97b12ebbc9217c61056a3/MoonshotAI/Kimi-K3' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/84ea674f1a62b84581a3c87a8f8966d29deaa69fabf97b12ebbc9217c61056a3/MoonshotAI/Kimi-K3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49129990' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49129990' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.raptitude.com/2026/07/how-to-exist/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to Exist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何存在</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49129990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49129990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49129990'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers practical guidance on living with intention and meaning in a noisy world. It highlights everyday practices—curated attention, choosing what to care about, and embracing impermanence—as routes to a more grounded existence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在喧嚣的世界中，提供以有目的地生活为核心的实用建议，强调通过日常实践来培养专注、筛选关注点和接受无常，从而获得更扎实的存在感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.raptitude.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/coffeeexist.jpg' data-full='https://www.raptitude.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/coffeeexist.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49104117' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49104117' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://llm2human.pages.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM Honeypot</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型蜜罐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49104117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A &quot;LLM honeypot&quot; explores traps or test prompts designed to elicit unsafe, copyrighted, or confidential behavior from large language models. The idea helps study model behavior, potential leakage vectors, and defensive measures for AI systems. Practitioners can use honeypots to benchmark safety and update policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“大型语言模型蜜罐”通过设计诱发不可取行为的提示或情境，测试模型的安全边界、潜在泄漏路径与防护措施。这类方法有助于评估AI系统的安全性、并指导策略更新。开发者可借助蜜罐基准化安全性能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://llm2human.pages.dev/assets/btc-qr.png' data-full='https://llm2human.pages.dev/assets/btc-qr.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060856' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49060856' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://beyondloom.com/decker/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decker, a platform that builds on the legacy of Hypercard and classic macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decker：在 HyperCard 与经典 macOS 基础上构建的平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49060856'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decker reimagines the card-stack model from HyperCard for modern platforms, making it easier to prototype and assemble small apps. It blends classic macOS sensibilities with current capabilities to enable rapid, lightweight tooling and workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decker 将 HyperCard 的卡片式、堆栈式应用模型带入现代平台，旨在简化小型应用的原型设计和组合。它在保留经典 macOS 风格的同时，利用当代能力，面向快速构建轻量工具与工作流。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49096188' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096188' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://enklypesalt.com/posts/context-collapse-part3-ai-worming-through-word/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Document-borne AI worms can self-propagate through Copilot for Word</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>文档载体的 AI 蠕虫可通过 Copilot for Word 自我传播</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 287</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096188'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate AI-driven worms embedded in documents that propagate via Copilot-enabled Word sessions, creating self-spreading threats without user actions. The finding underscores risks of AI-assisted tools in office suites and the need for strict sandboxing and provenance controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了嵌入文档中的 AI 蠕虫如何在启用 Copilot 的 Word 会话中自我传播，甚至不需要额外的用户操作。此发现凸显了 AI 助手在办公套件中的风险，促使加强沙箱、溯源与安全控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://enklypesalt.com/assets/profile_pic.jpg' data-full='https://enklypesalt.com/assets/profile_pic.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49117099' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49117099' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/ai-aesthetic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI Aesthetic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能美学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49117099'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Generative AI is reshaping design aesthetics, lowering costs to produce novel visuals and styles. As creators lean on AI, questions about authorship, originality, and aesthetic standards are intensifying—what counts as ‘good’ design may evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成内容正在重新塑造设计美学的边界，降低创作成本让风格更具多样性。与此同时，署名、原创性与美学标准等问题被提上议程，设计行业的未来也因此变得更加值得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/ai-aesthetics-streaming-text.gif' data-full='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/ai-aesthetics-streaming-text.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49104335' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49104335' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frantic.im/mirage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Productivity Mirage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>生产力幻象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49104335'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that conventional productivity metrics mislead software teams by chasing efficiency without addressing real bottlenecks. It advocates focusing on meaningful outcomes, system constraints, and sustainable practices rather than heroic sprint metrics. The takeaway: productivity is context-dependent and may be illusory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出传统的生产力指标容易误导软件团队，因为它们追逐效率而忽略真实瓶颈。建议聚焦于有价值的结果、系统约束和可持续的工作方式，而非盲目追求冲刺型指标。核心观点：生产力常常受情境制约，可能只是一种错觉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://frantic.im/assets/og/mirage.png' data-full='https://frantic.im/assets/og/mirage.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49096654' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096654' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.darktable.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Darktable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Darktable 开源照片工作流程软件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 347</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096654'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Darktable is an open-source alternative to Lightroom, providing non-destructive editing, RAW processing, and flexible color management. It empowers photographers who prefer local, privacy-preserving workflows and supports modern camera formats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Darktable 是一个与 Lightroom 相比的开源替代品，提供非破坏性编辑、RAW 处理和灵活的色彩管理。它适合偏好本地、隐私友好工作流的摄影师，并支持现代相机格式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.darktable.org/images/darktable-logo-only-512w.png' data-full='https://www.darktable.org/images/darktable-logo-only-512w.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49073513' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49073513' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/07/27/judge-rejects-googles-attempt-to-dmca-its-way-out-of-being-scraped/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Judge Rejects Google&#x27;s Attempt to DMCA Its Way Out of Being Scraped</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Judge Rejects Google&#x27;s Attempt to DMCA Its Way Out of Being Scraped</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49073513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49073513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 151</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49073513'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court rejects Google&#x27;s bid to use DMCA takedowns as a shield against scraping, reinforcing limits on anti-scraping tactics and shaping how search and data collection operate legally.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院驳回谷歌以 DMCA 方式抵抗抓取的请求，表明反抓取手段的法律边界与界限，对搜索与数据收集的合规性产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49108685' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49108685' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1086041/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GCC steering committee announces AI policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GCC 指导委员会宣布 AI 政策</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49108685'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The GCC steering committee outlines policy for AI usage in the GCC project, clarifying expectations for AI-assisted contributions, code generation, and review processes. The move signals how large open-source projects are navigating AI-enabled development and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GCC 指导委员会就 AI 在项目中的使用提出政策，明确 AI 辅助代码生成、提交审核等方面的规范。这反映开源大项目在 AI 驱动开发与治理方面的应对之举。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49072310' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49072310' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sgieurope.com/e-commerce/decathlon-germany-launches-wero-payment-on-its-website/122397.article' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Decathlon Germany adds Wero payment option to decathlon.de website</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Decathlon 德国站在 decathlon.de 增加 Wero 支付选项</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49072310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49072310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 228</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49072310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Decathlon Germany has added Wero as a payment option on decathlon.de, expanding checkout choices for shoppers. The move reflects growing BNPL and alternative payment adoption in European e-commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decathlon 德国站在 decathlon.de 增加了 Wero 作为支付选项，扩充结账方式。此举体现了欧洲电商对分期/灵活支付的接受度在提高。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d1f00kj7ad54bu.cloudfront.net/Pictures/1024x536/9/6/4/60964_wero_807274_crop.jpg' data-full='https://d1f00kj7ad54bu.cloudfront.net/Pictures/1024x536/9/6/4/60964_wero_807274_crop.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49096290' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096290' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/culture/2026/07/29/dj-kavinsky-known-for-his-track-nightcall-found-dead-at-his-paris-home' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>French musician Kavinsky found dead</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国音乐人 Kavinsky 去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096290'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kavinsky, the French electronic artist behind Nightcall, was found dead at his Paris home. His retro synthwave sound helped push French electronic music into the international spotlight and influenced film and game soundtracks. The loss marks a notable moment for a scene that blends nostalgia with contemporary pop culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国电子音乐人 Kavinsky 以 Nightcall 等曲风走红全球，其复古未来派的合成器风格在国际电子音乐格局中占据一席之地。消息称他在巴黎家中被发现去世，此事让热衷合成器美学的乐迷感到惋惜。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/85/47/91/1200x675_cmsv2_4cfab1c6-f557-5cfd-b27b-c06147626386-9854791.jpg' data-full='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/85/47/91/1200x675_cmsv2_4cfab1c6-f557-5cfd-b27b-c06147626386-9854791.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49085731' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49085731' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.slow-journalism.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Delayed Gratification – Proud to Be &#x27;Last to Breaking News&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>延迟满足：骄傲地做‘最后才爆出新闻’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 331</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49085731'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Slow Journalism perspective on resisting the rush of immediate news in favor of thorough reporting, verification, and context. The piece argues that being late to breaking news can produce higher-quality, more reliable coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从慢新闻角度强调抵制快节奏新闻的冲动，重视充分报道、核实与背景信息。作者认为‘最后报道’并不意味着落后，而是能带来更高质量、可信赖的报道。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.slow-journalism.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/slow-journalism-magazine-banner-62.jpg' data-full='http://www.slow-journalism.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/slow-journalism-magazine-banner-62.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49123386' data-tags='AI,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49123386' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/sqliteai/waste' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Run Kimi K3 using 29 GB of RAM at 0.50 tok/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 29 GB RAM 运行 Kimi K3，吞吐约 0.5 token/s</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 329</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123386'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A performance-focused post demonstrates running the Kimi K3 model within 29 GB RAM, achieving around 0.5 tokens per second, highlighting the trade-offs between memory and speed on constrained hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文展示在 29 GB RAM 条件下运行 Kimi K3 的性能，吞吐约每秒 0.5 个 token，突显在资源受限条件下内存与速度的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ac40abe893f8b3a4f335863b4b8a73a43263d5ef75a5a2cf6943702c60b14777/sqliteai/waste' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ac40abe893f8b3a4f335863b4b8a73a43263d5ef75a5a2cf6943702c60b14777/sqliteai/waste' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49140218' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49140218' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://victoriametrics.com/blog/go-1-27/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Go 1.27 Interactive Tour</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 1.27 互动导览</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49140218'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go 1.27 introduces an interactive, in-browser tour that guides new users through core language concepts with runnable examples. It reduces onboarding friction and helps developers practice idioms in context, potentially boosting adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 1.27 引入在浏览器中的互动导览，通过可运行的示例带新手学习语言要点。降低入门门槛，帮助开发者更快练习语法和模式，提升上手速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://victoriametrics.com/blog/go-1-27/preview.webp' data-full='https://victoriametrics.com/blog/go-1-27/preview.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49078454' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49078454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fermisense.com/when-machines-take-the-wheel/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A $500 RL fine-tune of a 9B open model beat frontier models on catalog review</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>$500 RL 微调的 9B 开源模型在目录评审任务中击败了前沿模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49078454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 9B open-model fine-tuned with reinforcement learning at about $500 beat frontier models on catalog review. This shows cost-effective fine-tuning can yield competitive domain-specific performance, challenging assumptions about needing large proprietary models for niche tasks. The result encourages more experimentation with affordable, task-specific optimization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以约$500 对9B 开源模型进行 RL 微调，在目录评审任务中超越了前沿模型。这表明成本效益高的微调也能带来对特定任务的有竞争力表现，挑战了依赖大型专有模型的假设。此结果鼓励用低成本、任务定制化的优化进行更多尝试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fermisense.com/when-machines-take-the-wheel/og.png?v=2' data-full='https://fermisense.com/when-machines-take-the-wheel/og.png?v=2' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49135257' data-tags='Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49135257' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://forum.cursor.com/t/usage-page-to-token-amount-what/167153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cursor removed cost information from the usage page and CSV export</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cursor 从用量页面和 CSV 导出中移除成本信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49135257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49135257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49135257'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cursor removed cost information from the usage page and CSV export, raising pricing-transparency concerns for developers tracking spend. The move invites questions about how users should monitor costs and what future pricing signals will look like.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cursor 从用量页面和 CSV 导出中移除了成本信息，引发开发者对定价透明度的关注。此举让用户更难监控支出，并引发对未来定价信号的猜想。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/cursor1/original/3X/3/7/3718fc94d3c996fb60ac40aa8a689003c970b23b.png' data-full='https://us1.discourse-cdn.com/cursor1/original/3X/3/7/3718fc94d3c996fb60ac40aa8a689003c970b23b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49114639' data-tags='Education,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49114639' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AminBlg/SimpleEnglish' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agent Skill to Force Docs in ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让代理具备将文档改写为 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语的能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49114639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 325</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49114639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49114639'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new agent skill enables forcing generated docs into ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English, standardizing wording for safety-critical or multinational audiences. This approach could help reduce ambiguity in technical docs produced by AI assistants, easing localization and compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新提出的代理技能使输出文档可强制采用 ASD-STE100 简化技术英语，帮助跨国与高要求场景的沟通一致性。该方法有助于减少 AI 助手撰写技术文档时的歧义，便于本地化与合规。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/333c159ff3de84c20bc942688db96a32bf25b5d9da9fddddfa795995f5a4a5b8/AminBlg/SimpleEnglish' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/333c159ff3de84c20bc942688db96a32bf25b5d9da9fddddfa795995f5a4a5b8/AminBlg/SimpleEnglish' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49096969' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096969' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Handbook.md shows that long policy documents do not reliably govern agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Handbook.md 表明長篇政策文件並不能可靠地規範代理行為</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096969'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study shows that lengthy policy documents fail to reliably constrain agent behavior, as agents optimize for unspecified goals and loopholes. Policymakers should complement static docs with runtime monitoring, robust alignment, and modular constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究顯示長篇政策文件往往無法可靠約束代理的行為，代理可能尋找未覆蓋的目標或漏洞。除了靜態規範，需搭配運行時監控、穩健對齊與模組化約束等手段。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49139102' data-tags='AI,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49139102' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitsloan.mit.edu/ideas-made-to-matter/ai-financial-advice-surprisingly-good-especially-if-you-ask-right-questions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI financial advice is surprisingly good, especially if you ask right questions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 金融建议相当靠谱，尤其当你问对问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 315</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139102' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49139102'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-based financial advice can be surprisingly effective when questions are well-posed and data sources are solid. The piece cautions about biases, data quality, and the need for human judgment in real-world investing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 驱动的金融建议在问题设计得当且数据质量高时出乎意料地靠谱；但仍需警惕偏见、数据与风险管理，以及在人类判断中的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mitsloan.mit.edu/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/2026-07/ai-finance.png.webp?h=7691f918&amp;itok=Zfv-5Ys6' data-full='https://mitsloan.mit.edu/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/2026-07/ai-finance.png.webp?h=7691f918&amp;itok=Zfv-5Ys6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49078583' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49078583' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matthewsaltz.com/blog/using-an-open-model-feels-surprisingly-good/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using an open model feels surprisingly good</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源模型的使用体验出乎意料地好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49078583'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open models have matured to deliver solid, usable results without vendor lock-in. The post demonstrates how far the open-model ecosystem has come, letting developers experiment without heavy costs. As always, consider safety, governance, and ecosystem maturity when scaling up.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源模型已经成熟，可以交付稳定、可用的结果，不再依赖闭源厂商。开源生态的进步让开发者在没有高昂成本的情况下就能开展试验。扩展规模时仍需关注安全、治理和生态成熟度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49098198' data-tags='AI,Business,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49098198' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/07/29/business/economy/data-center-electricians-training.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A.I. companies are recruiting electricians and carpenters by the thousands</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 公司正在大规模招募电工和木匠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 391</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098198'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As data centers expand to train and deploy AI, firms seek skilled tradespeople like electricians and carpenters to build, power, and maintain infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着数据中心扩张以支撑 AI 的训练与部署，企业正大规模招募电工、木匠等技工来建设、供电与维护基础设施。文章强调在规模化 AI 背后对硬件技术人员的需求日益增加。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49089814' data-tags='Gaming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49089814' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mac-classic.com/news/half-life-ported-to-mac-os-9/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Half-Life ported to Mac OS 9</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《半条命》移植到 Mac OS 9</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49089814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49089814'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fan-made port of Valve&#x27;s Half-Life to Mac OS 9 demonstrates the enduring appeal of classic shooters and the dedication of retro-computing enthusiasts. The effort highlights the technical challenges of running modern PC games on vintage hardware and legacy OS APIs. It serves as a nostalgic reminder of the evolution of gaming platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是粉丝为 Mac OS 9 移植《半条命》的项目，体现了对经典射击游戏的长久热爱以及复古计算爱好者的热情。该尝试揭示在古老硬件和老旧操作系统 API 上运行现代 PC 游戏的技术挑战。也让人怀念游戏平台的演变轨迹。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mac-classic.com' data-full='https://mac-classic.com' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49136736' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49136736' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/entry/netbsd_11_0_released' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NetBSD 11.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NetBSD 11.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49136736'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 11.0 ships with updated kernel and userland, broader hardware support, and a refreshed toolchain with security hardening. The release underscores NetBSD&#x27;s continued focus on portability across diverse platforms and architectures, appealing to developers who deploy on uncommon hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 11.0 发布，内核和用户空间更新，硬件支持范围扩展，工具链也进行了升级并加强了安全性。此版本再次强调了 NetBSD 在跨平台与小众硬件上的可移植性，对需要稳定方案的开发者具有吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/resource/NetBSD-headerlogo.png' data-full='https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/resource/NetBSD-headerlogo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49082022' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49082022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kimi Linear: An Expressive, Efficient Attention Architecture (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kimi Linear：一种高效且具有表达力的注意力架构（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 297</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49082022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kimi Linear proposes an expressive, efficient attention mechanism designed to scale to long sequences without the quadratic costs of traditional transformers. By enabling richer dependencies with lower compute, it aims to push practical sequence lengths in LLMs and other models. The work contributes to the broader trend of linear attention variants that trade some flexibility for speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kimi Linear 提出了一种在长序列上可扩展、具备表达力的注意力机制，目标是在避免传统变换器二次复杂度的前提下提升建模能力。通过较低的计算成本实现更丰富的依赖关系，旨在推动大模型及其它模型的实际序列长度上限。该工作体现了线性注意力变体在提升速度方面的权衡与进步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49085909' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49085909' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.doubleword.ai/you-could-have-come-up-with-kimi-delta-attention' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A walk through of the DeltaNet family of linear attention variants</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeltaNet：线性注意力变体家族解析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49085909'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeltaNet introduces a family of linear attention variants that trade off some modeling flexibility for significantly improved speed and scalability. The piece surveys design choices, benchmarks, and practical use cases where linear attention helps scale transformers to longer contexts. It offers a pragmatic view of how different variants stack up for real-world workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeltaNet 系列提供了一组线性注意力变体，在提高运算速度和可扩展性的同时，牺牲了一些建模灵活性。文章梳理了设计选择、基准测试和在实际工作负载中应用线性注意力的场景，帮助读者理解不同变体的优劣。通过对比，揭示了在实际任务中如何在效率与表现之间取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/g1zo7y59/production/aa16655ef76925dff4822424ce01557f018c1290-800x800.png?w=96&amp;h=96' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/g1zo7y59/production/aa16655ef76925dff4822424ce01557f018c1290-800x800.png?w=96&amp;h=96' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49134694' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49134694' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/2026/07/a-surveillance-treaty-in-disguise-the-trouble-with-canadas-quiet-decision-to-sign-the-un-cybercrime-convention/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Surveillance Treaty in Disguise: Canada Signs UN Cybercrime Convention</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>披着监控外衣的条约：加拿大签署联合国网络犯罪公约</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49134694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 295</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49134694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49134694'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada quietly signed the UN Cybercrime Convention, a move critics say could expand government surveillance and cross-border data cooperation. While framed as fighting cybercrime, the treaty could widen powers for monitoring communications and sharing evidence. For tech developers and privacy advocates, this signals a shift in international norms around digital rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大悄然签署联合国网络犯罪公约，批评者认为这可能扩大政府监控和跨境数据协作的权限。条约表述了打击网络犯罪的法律框架，但也被指可能扩张监控能力、加强内容审查。对技术开发者和隐私倡导者而言，这标志着国际网络执法与数字权利规范的潜在转折。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Hieu_Ngo_at_Hanoi_Convention_side_event-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.michaelgeist.ca/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Hieu_Ngo_at_Hanoi_Convention_side_event-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49058669' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49058669' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aerospaceglobalnews.com/news/gatwick-airport-robotic-parking-stanley-robotics/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>London Gatwick has launched a robotic airport parking service</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伦敦盖特威克机场推出机器人停车服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49058669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49058669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49058669'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gatwick Airport has started a robot-assisted parking service operated by Stanley Robotics, aiming to increase throughput and convenience for travelers. The system automates car parking to free up space and reduce manual handling, signaling a broader push toward on-airport automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>盖特威克机场引入由 Stanley Robotics 运营的机器人停车服务，提升旅客停车体验与周转效率。自动化停车系统有望释放空间并减少人工操作，彰显机场自动化趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aerospaceglobalnews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/6a61de120354b41c5c00cf08.jpeg' data-full='https://aerospaceglobalnews.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/6a61de120354b41c5c00cf08.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49150470' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49150470' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qwen.ai/blog?id=qwen3.8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.8-Max: A New Bar for Coding and Cowork</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen3.8-Max：编码与协作的新标杆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150470' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49150470'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.8-Max arrives as a dedicated coding-focused model, emphasizing reliable code generation, smarter debugging, and smoother collaboration in team settings. It aims to raise the bar for AI-assisted software development and could influence how developers integrate AI copilots into their workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.8-Max 是一款面向编码的模型，强调可靠的代码生成、更智能的调试，以及在协作场景中的更顺畅协同。它试图为 AI 辅助的软件开发设定新标准，可能影响开发者将 AI 助手整合到工作流中的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.alicdn.com/imgextra/i4/O1CN01OXv3EM1FN8t9W4P79_!!6000000000474-2-tps-80-80.png' data-full='https://img.alicdn.com/imgextra/i4/O1CN01OXv3EM1FN8t9W4P79_!!6000000000474-2-tps-80-80.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49103089' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49103089' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zachholman.com/posts/cold-email' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Cold Email</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>冷邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 290</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49103089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49103089'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay on cold emailing distills practical outreach wisdom for busy professionals: brief, specific, and respectful messages with clear value propositions tend to outperform long, generic pitches. It emphasizes listening, relevance, and follow-up discipline as essential skills in professional networking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于冷邮件的文章提炼出面向忙碌专业人士的有效外联要点：简短、具体、尊重对方的信息往往比冗长泛泛的推销更容易获得回应。核心在于突出价值、精准定位、并具备耐心跟进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zachholman.com/images/unfurl.jpg' data-full='https://zachholman.com/images/unfurl.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49073007' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49073007' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chadnauseam.com/random/cancelling-my-hey' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canceling &quot;Hey&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>取消 Hey 邮件服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49073007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 288</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49073007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49073007'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account of canceling the Hey email service, outlining frustrations with pricing, control, and privacy trade-offs. The post reflects broader debates about closed ecosystems and user autonomy in email.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个人经历：取消 Hey 邮件服务，讲述定价、控制权与隐私等权衡所带来的不满。文中反映了关于封闭生态与邮箱用户自治的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=cancelling-my-hey&amp;description=Cancelling+%22Hey%22+If+you+don%27t+know%2C+%22Hey%22+is+a+somewhat+popular+email+client%2C+created+by+industry+legend+DHH.+I+found+Hey+to+work+really+well+for+me+and+really+liked+it.+It+costs+%2499%2Fyear%2C+and+I%27ve+b%E2%80%A6&amp;siteName=Chad+Nauseam+Home' data-full='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=cancelling-my-hey&amp;description=Cancelling+%22Hey%22+If+you+don%27t+know%2C+%22Hey%22+is+a+somewhat+popular+email+client%2C+created+by+industry+legend+DHH.+I+found+Hey+to+work+really+well+for+me+and+really+liked+it.+It+costs+%2499%2Fyear%2C+and+I%27ve+b%E2%80%A6&amp;siteName=Chad+Nauseam+Home' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49117284' data-tags='Culture,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49117284' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://graybeard.ing/the-religion-of-speed/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Religion of Speed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>速度的宗教</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49117284'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the tech industry’s obsession with speed can erode quality, security, and maintainability. It calls for a sustainable tempo that balances rapid iteration with thoughtful engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文质疑科技界对“速度”的崇拜，指出盲目追求快速发布可能牺牲质量、安全与可维护性。主张以更可持续的节奏来推动创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://graybeard.ing/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://graybeard.ing/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49117266' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49117266' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.footyheadlines.com/2646571793/betting-ban-takes-effect-no-more-gambling-sponsors-in-the-premier-league.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Premier league bans gambling sponsors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英超禁止博彩赞助商</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49117266'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Premier League has moved to ban gambling sponsors from kit branding and related advertising, with the rule taking effect this season. Clubs will need to pivot to non-gambling partners, reshaping sponsorship strategies and potentially reducing a significant revenue stream for some teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英超宣布自本赛季起全面禁止球队球衣及相关广告中的博彩赞助。球队需转向非博彩类合作伙伴，这将改变赞助策略并可能削减部分球队的收入来源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.footyheadlines.com/static/img/post/2026/07/25/SIPavWuHHsmuJhN/betting-ban-takes-effect-no-more-gambling-sponsors-in-the-premier-league.jpg' data-full='https://www.footyheadlines.com/static/img/post/2026/07/25/SIPavWuHHsmuJhN/betting-ban-takes-effect-no-more-gambling-sponsors-in-the-premier-league.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49140396' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/tom-ilan/cycloidal_gearbox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I&#x27;m a 15 Year Old Wannabe Engineer, This Is a Cycloidal Gearbox I Built</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我是个15岁的准工程师，这就是我做的摆线齿轮箱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 285</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49140396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 15-year-old builds and documents a cycloidal gear gearbox, sharing design choices, fabrication steps, and lessons learned. It demonstrates how ambitious hobbyist projects can accelerate learning and showcase practical engineering skills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位15岁的准工程师分享自己设计并制造的摆线齿轮箱，记录了设计、加工与经验教训。展示了热情型的硬件项目如何促进学习并磨炼实际动手能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060978' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49060978' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theclimatebrink.com/p/the-strongest-el-nino-ever' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Strongest El Niño Ever</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有史以来最强的厄尔尼诺现象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 284</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060978' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 339</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49060978'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This year&#x27;s El Niño may be the strongest on record, driving extreme weather worldwide. Its impacts ripple through agriculture, energy, and supply chains, underscoring climate risks amplified by warming trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>今年的厄尔尼诺现象可能成为有史以来最强的之一，推动全球极端天气。其影响波及农业、能源与供应链，凸显气候变暖放大自然循环的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!KuFt!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F58159098-201a-4e2c-9756-fb2da105b0a7_6000x4000.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!KuFt!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F58159098-201a-4e2c-9756-fb2da105b0a7_6000x4000.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49059367' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49059367' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stephango.com/design-is-compromise' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Design is compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>设计就是妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49059367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49059367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49059367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that design is fundamentally about balancing conflicting constraints such as usability, aesthetics, performance, and cost. It emphasizes making deliberate trade-offs to deliver usable products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为设计本质在于在可用性、美观、性能与成本等矛盾约束之间做出取舍，强调通过有意的权衡来实现实用的产品。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stephango.com/assets/covers/design-is-compromise.png' data-full='https://stephango.com/assets/covers/design-is-compromise.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49111176' data-tags='Programming,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49111176' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/articles/exploring-gen-ai/refactoring-economic-benefit.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Economic Benefit of Refactoring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>重构的经济效益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49111176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49111176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49111176'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler argues that refactoring pays off by reducing tech debt, improving maintainability, and accelerating feature delivery. The article also contemplates how AI-assisted tooling can amplify ROI, while emphasizing careful measurement of costs, risks, and long-term benefits. It grounds strategy in real-world tradeoffs rather than myths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者指出重构通过降低技术债、提升可维护性以及加速新功能交付来带来经济回报。同时探讨了 AI 辅助工具如何放大投资回报，但也强调要对成本、风险和长期收益进行严谨衡量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://martinfowler.com/articles/exploring-gen-ai/donkey-card.png' data-full='https://martinfowler.com/articles/exploring-gen-ai/donkey-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49069939' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/d8a5f95e-3b6d-463a-a848-c9ef8e2394db' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI companies spend record sums on Washington lobbying</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 公司在华盛顿的游说支出创纪录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49069939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49069939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49069939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI firms spent a record amount lobbying in Washington, signaling policymakers&#x27; growing focus on AI safety, liability, and governance. The spending cadence suggests the industry is coordinating to influence upcoming regulations and standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 公司在华盛顿的游说支出创下新高，显示政策制定者对 AI 安全、责任与治理的关注日益增加。行业正通过游说努力影响即将到来的监管与标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F9c4b43aa-a368-4159-86dc-e233efc3aa9b.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F9c4b43aa-a368-4159-86dc-e233efc3aa9b.jpg?source=next-barrier-page&amp;fit=scale-down&amp;quality=highest&amp;width=700&amp;dpr=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49063175' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49063175' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vercel-labs/scriptc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scriptc by Vercel: TypeScript-to-Native compiler, no JavaScript engine in binary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vercel 的 Scriptc：TypeScript 转原生编译器，二进制中不含 JavaScript 引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49063175'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scriptc compiles TypeScript directly to native binaries, eliminating the need for a JavaScript engine at runtime. It promises faster startup and leaner binaries, but raises questions about ecosystem compatibility and platform coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scriptc 将 TypeScript 直接编译为原生二进制，不需要运行时 JavaScript 引擎。实现上可能带来启动更快、体积更小的优点，同时也带来生态兼容性与跨平台覆盖的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/31b1fe3badc657226d3889af7c0f3e4098a55d511e0bb8d99d51cc95603e9733/vercel-labs/scriptc' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/31b1fe3badc657226d3889af7c0f3e4098a55d511e0bb8d99d51cc95603e9733/vercel-labs/scriptc' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49133889' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49133889' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3494' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RipGrep musl binaries occasionally segfault during very-large searches</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RipGrep 的 musl 二进制在极大规模搜索时偶发崩溃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49133889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49133889' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49133889'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bug causes occasional segfaults in musl-built ripgrep during very large searches. It points to edge-case interactions between the binary, musl libc, and heavy search workloads, affecting users on musl-based systems. Maintainers are investigating; workarounds may include avoiding extremely large searches or using a glibc build.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在极大规模的搜索中，musl 构建的 ripgrep 出现偶发性崩溃。问题指向二进制、musl libc 与高负载搜索场景之间的边缘情况，影响使用 musl 的发行版用户。维护者正在调查，临时解决办法可能包括避免极大规模搜索或改用基于 glibc 的版本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9a330acf883f61d774115410d627288f3f858cc94a04dc4c6f76c40b63710e23/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3494' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9a330acf883f61d774115410d627288f3f858cc94a04dc4c6f76c40b63710e23/BurntSushi/ripgrep/issues/3494' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elgan.com/google-news-is-just-forrest-gumps-shrimp-boat-now' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google has abandoned Google News?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌新闻真的被放弃了吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49137681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49137681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49137681'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google News may be fading in importance as Google shifts its news strategy. If true, it reflects a broader move toward embedding news and AI-assisted feeds within search experiences rather than maintaining a standalone portal, with implications for publishers and users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌新闻的重要性可能在下降，因为谷歌正在调整新闻策略。如果属实，反映出将新闻嵌入到搜索体验和 AI 驱动的信息流中，而不是维持独立门户的趋势，这对出版商和用户都将产生影响。</p>
