<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cryptographyengineering.com/2026/07/29/some-notes-about-anthropics-new-results/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Some thoughts about Anthropic&#x27;s new cryptanalysis results</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于 Anthropic 新的密码分析结果的一些想法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49099804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49099804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49099804'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cryptography-focused take on Anthropic&#x27;s latest findings highlights how new analyses challenge assumptions about model security and robustness. The piece argues for more rigorous evaluation and transparent threat models in AI safety research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本篇从密码学角度评析 Anthropic 的最新发现，强调新的分析可能挑战对模型安全性和鲁棒性的既有假设。文章呼吁在 AI 安全研究中采用更严格的评估与透明的威胁模型。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49097961' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49097961' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stackoverflow.blog/2026/07/29/developers-are-attached-to-tools-because-tools-encode-trust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Developers are attached to tools because tools encode trust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发者依赖工具，因为工具编码信任</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49097961'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers grow attached to tools because those tools encode trust—promises about behavior, reliability, and security. Once trust is established, teams keep using the same tools even as new options emerge, shaping workflows and ecosystem choices. The piece reflects on how tooling design subtly steers engineering decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发者之所以依赖工具，是因为工具把信任编码进去——对行为、可靠性与安全性的承诺。一旦建立信任，团队就会继续使用相同的工具，即使出现新的替代方案，也会被工作流和生态系统取向所左右。文章反映了工具设计如何在潜移默化中引导工程决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.stackoverflow.co/images/jo7n4k8s/production/29bbae9c01de534b803084e4f0b9a77f9cd90e5c-12000x6300.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.stackoverflow.co/images/jo7n4k8s/production/29bbae9c01de534b803084e4f0b9a77f9cd90e5c-12000x6300.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49085236' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49085236' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/news/grapheneos-defends-data-wiping-function-that-blocked-us-border-search' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS Defends Data-Wiping Function That Blocked US Border Search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS 为阻止边境搜查的数据清除功能辩护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49085236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS defends a data-wiping feature that allegedly blocks border searches, arguing it protects user privacy and device security in sensitive travel. The feature remains controversial, drawing attention to the tension between privacy tools and law enforcement. The story highlights ongoing debates over mobile OS design choices in high-stakes environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 为一项数据清除功能辩护，该功能被认为能在敏感旅行情景下阻止边境搜查、保护用户隐私与设备安全。该功能仍具争议，凸显隐私工具与执法之间的紧张关系。此事反映出在关键情境下，移动操作系统设计选择所引发的辩论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/04Q5eYku2OgeBOOGiL3i0i8-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1785173872.jpg' data-full='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/04Q5eYku2OgeBOOGiL3i0i8-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1785173872.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/0f9b2fe7-bde4-4f5f-b49e-93ccb5da9ea8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Treasury undertakes historic intervention in yen market</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国财政部对日元市场进行历史性干预</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49143188'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. Treasury’s intervention in the yen market marks a rare direct action in currency markets, signaling heightened concern about volatility and potential policy coordination with other central banks. The move could influence FX liquidity, risk premia, and global funding dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美财政部对日元市场的干预是罕见的直接行动，体现对波动的担忧及可能与其他央行的协同。此举可能影响外汇流动性、风险溢价及全球融资格局。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A directory of people who love RSS, highlighting the enduring value of RSS feeds amid a crowded content landscape. It underscores that a small but dedicated community still champions decentralized, text-based syndication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个公开表达对 RSS 的热爱者名录，强调 RSS 订阅在喧嚣的内容生态中的持久价值。指出尽管主流趋势向 AI 内容推荐靠拢，仍有一个小而专注的社群坚持去中心化的文本订阅。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://getbor.dev/blog/2026-08-02-bor-v080-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Bor – Open-source policy management for Linux desktops</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49142569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49142569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49142569'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bor is an open-source policy management tool for Linux desktops, aimed at standardizing security and user settings across devices. It provides policy templates and a centralized approach to desktop administration for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bor 是一个面向 Linux 桌面端的开源策略管理工具，旨在在多设备之间统一安全与用户设置。它提供策略模板与集中化的桌面管理。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49082695' data-tags='Business,Culture,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49082695' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mondragon_Corporation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mondragon Corporation – a federation of co-operatives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蒙德拉贡集团——一个合作社联合体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49082695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49082695'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mondragon Corporation is a longstanding federation of worker cooperatives in the Basque region, notable for its cooperative governance, social mission, and diversified businesses. The model demonstrates how scale, education, and mutual aid can sustain worker ownership across industries. Analysts view it as a unique alternative to traditional capitalist structures with lessons for tech worker ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蒙德拉贡集团是西班牙巴斯克地区的工人合作社联合体，以共同治理、社会使命和多元化业务著称。其模式展示了规模化、教育与互助如何在各行业维持员工所有权。对于技术行业的从业者而言，这一案例提供了关于员工所有权的独特启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/cf/Viaje_a_la_Corporaci%C3%B3n_Mondrag%C3%B3n_en_el_Pa%C3%ADs_Vasco.jpg/1280px-Viaje_a_la_Corporaci%C3%B3n_Mondrag%C3%B3n_en_el_Pa%C3%ADs_Vasco.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/c/cf/Viaje_a_la_Corporaci%C3%B3n_Mondrag%C3%B3n_en_el_Pa%C3%ADs_Vasco.jpg/1280px-Viaje_a_la_Corporaci%C3%B3n_Mondrag%C3%B3n_en_el_Pa%C3%ADs_Vasco.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AARomanov1985/Audio-Cassette-Simulation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 FFmpeg 模拟磁带音频特性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49061887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49061887' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49061887'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A GitHub project demonstrates how to simulate cassette tape audio profiles using FFmpeg filters, capturing characteristics like frequency response and hiss. This enables retro-audio enthusiasts and preservation work to reproduce authentic analog textures in digital workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 GitHub 项目展示如何用 FFmpeg 过滤器模拟磁带音频特性，包含频响、嘶声等模拟参数。对于复古音频爱好者和档案保护工作，这为在数字工作流中再现真实的磁带质感提供了工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/64086e695a95339799037f5e0d12051d65a215ce1c1f333ae03e26d5d18b79fd/AARomanov1985/Audio-Cassette-Simulation' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/64086e695a95339799037f5e0d12051d65a215ce1c1f333ae03e26d5d18b79fd/AARomanov1985/Audio-Cassette-Simulation' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49143925' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49143925' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fstar-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F*: A general-purpose proof-oriented programming language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>F*：通用的、以证明为导向的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49143925'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>F* is a general-purpose, proof-oriented programming language with a ML-like syntax and dependent types. It enables writing verifiable software, with abilities to extract code to OCaml or F#, making it relevant for security-critical applications and formal verification efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>F* 是一种通用、以证明为导向的编程语言，采用 ML 风格语法并支持依赖类型。它可编写可验证的软件，并能提取为 OCaml/F#，在安全关键应用与形式化验证领域具有价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fstar-lang.org/i/fstar-new.png' data-full='https://fstar-lang.org/i/fstar-new.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.historytoday.com/archive/portrait-author-historian/absurdity-albert-camus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Absurdity of Albert Camus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔贝·加缪的荒诞性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49117089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49117089'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines Camus&#x27;s notion of the absurd and why it remains influential in contemporary culture. The piece ties the philosophy to questions of meaning, ethics, and resilience in the face of uncertainty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨加缪荒诞哲学的要义及其在当代文化中的持续影响。文章将这一思想与关于意义、伦理和在不确定性中保持韧性的议题联系起来。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49092112' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49092112' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blinry.org/multi-seat-wayland/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>State of multi-player Wayland</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wayland 的多席实现现状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49092112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49092112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49092112'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Multi-seat support on Wayland enables multiple independent users on one machine, useful for shared workstations, labs, and kiosks. Current status varies by compositor and GPU driver, with ongoing work on input multiplexing, seat management, and security isolation. The evolution highlights a shift from traditional X11-style multi-user setups toward modern, compositor-driven architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wayland 的多席功能允许一台机器上存在多位独立用户，适用于共享工作站、实验室或自助终端。不同合成器和显卡驱动的支持程度各异，输入设备分配、座席管理和安全隔离等方面仍在持续改进。随着生态演进，这一方向体现了从传统 X11 的多用户模式向现代合成器驱动架构的转变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blinry.org/multi-seat-wayland/thumbnail.jpg-thumbnail.jpg' data-full='https://blinry.org/multi-seat-wayland/thumbnail.jpg-thumbnail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49020993' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49020993' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cen.acs.org/materials/adhesives/glue-bonds-nonstick-surfaces-wipes-clean/104/web/2026/07' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Glue bonds to nonstick surfaces and wipes clean with ethanol</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>能在不粘表面粘合并可用乙醇清洗的胶粘剂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49020993'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A glue formulation bonds to fluoropolymer nonstick surfaces and can be wiped away with ethanol. This enables temporary adhesion on slick substrates and residue-free removal, potentially useful for repairs on coated components or delicate assemblies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种胶粘剂能在聚四氟乙烯等不粘表面上实现牢固粘结，并可用乙醇擦洗清除。这在光滑基材上的临时粘接与无残留清除方面具有潜在应用，适用于涂层部件或精密装配的修复。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/image/CENODS/News--Powerful-adhesive-binds-to-Teflon--can-be-wiped-clean---534105:Widescreen?$responsive$&amp;qlt=90,0&amp;resMode=sharp2&amp;fmt=webp' data-full='https://s7d1.scene7.com/is/image/CENODS/News--Powerful-adhesive-binds-to-Teflon--can-be-wiped-clean---534105:Widescreen?$responsive$&amp;qlt=90,0&amp;resMode=sharp2&amp;fmt=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49053708' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49053708' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.seti.org/news/alien-world-chemistry-found-inside-meteorite/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alien World Chemistry Found Inside Meteorite That Struck New Jersey Home</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>坠落新泽西的陨石中的外星世界化学</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49053708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49053708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49053708'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Analyses of a meteorite that struck a New Jersey home reveal chemical signatures consistent with materials from other planetary bodies, shedding light on the diversity of early solar system chemistry. The finding highlights how meteorites preserve exotic minerals and isotopes that inform planetary science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对坠落在新泽西住宅的陨石进行分析后，研究人员发现符合来自其他星体的化学成分证据，揭示早期太阳系材料的多样性。此发现强调陨石如何保存外星化学信息，助力行星科学研究。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.seti.org/media/hk4lcjsh/figure-4.jpg' data-full='https://www.seti.org/media/hk4lcjsh/figure-4.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49110547' data-tags='Culture,Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hackerpublicradio.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hacker Public Radio</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hacker Public Radio</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49110547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-running podcast network focused on hacker culture, security, and open-source topics. It features tutorials, discussions, and community voices, serving as a practical archive for learnings in security and software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个长期运行的播客网络，聚焦黑客文化、安全与开源主题，涵盖教程、讨论与社区声音，成为安全与软件学习的实践性档案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hackerpublicradio.org/images/icons/mic-fill.svg' data-full='https://hackerpublicradio.org/images/icons/mic-fill.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49090867' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49090867' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scripps.ucsd.edu/news/underwater-oxygen-loss-threatens-earths-stability-researchers-warn' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Underwater oxygen loss threatens earth&#x27;s stability, researchers warn</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海洋缺氧正在威胁地球系统的稳定性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49090867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49090867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49090867'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers warn that ocean deoxygenation could threaten climate stability by disrupting marine ecosystems and carbon cycling, with expanding oxygen minimum zones affecting biodiversity and nutrient dynamics. If trends continue, consequences may include changes in sea life distribution and potential feedbacks on atmospheric composition. The findings underscore the urgency of monitoring and mitigating ocean health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员警告，海洋缺氧将扰乱海洋生态系统和碳循环，氧最小区的扩大将影响生物多样性和养分动态。若趋势持续，海洋生物分布及海气相互作用等方面可能出现反馈，进而影响大气组成。研究强调了监测和改善海洋健康的紧迫性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scripps.ucsd.edu/sites/default/files/styles/800px/public/2026-07/istock-177540653-800x500.jpg?itok=7zkOfvBW' data-full='https://scripps.ucsd.edu/sites/default/files/styles/800px/public/2026-07/istock-177540653-800x500.jpg?itok=7zkOfvBW' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49066928' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49066928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.videolan.org/videolan/vlc-unity' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VLC for Unity now supported on Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VLC for Unity 现已在 Linux 上支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49066928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49066928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49066928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VLC integration for Unity now supports Linux, enabling cross-platform media playback in Unity apps. This broadens multimedia capabilities for games and tools that rely on robust video features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VLC 与 Unity 的集成现在在 Linux 上得到支持，使 Unity 应用中的跨平台视频播放 becomes possible，扩展了对视频功能依赖的游戏与工具的多媒体能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://code.videolan.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' data-full='https://code.videolan.org/.within.website/x/cmd/anubis/static/img/pensive.webp?cacheBuster=1.25.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49140696' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49140696' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/linux/comments/1vcpk8i/linux_desktop_market_share_has_hit_over_10_in/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux desktop market share has hit over 10% in North America</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>北美 Linux 桌面市场份额突破 10%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49140696'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>North America&#x27;s Linux desktop share has crossed 10%, signaling growing adoption across distros among developers and enthusiasts. While notable, analysts caution about measurement methods and regional biases that can skew such figures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>北美地区 Linux 桌面市场份额突破 10%，显示开发者和爱好者对不同发行版的接受度提升。尽管意义显著，分析师也提醒关注统计口径与区域样本偏差可能扭曲数据。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49118663' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49118663' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/california-aquifer-may-have-crossed-point-no-return' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A California aquifer may have crossed the point of no return</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州一处含水层或已越过不可逆点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49118663'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research suggests a California aquifer may have passed a tipping point, threatening long-term water availability for agriculture and communities. Drier winters, overdrafting, and climate pressures intensify the resource squeeze.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州一处含水层可能已经越过不可逆的临界点，长期水资源供给面临威胁。干旱、过度抽取与气候压力叠加，使农业与社区将承受更大压力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49093845' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49093845' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://foldingglobes.com/globes' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Folding Paper Globes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>折叠纸球</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49093845'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Folding Paper Globes demonstrates constructing spherical globes through paper folding, blending geometric insight with hands-on craft. It’s a neat intersection of math, art, and education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《折叠纸球》通过折纸技术展示如何用纸张折出球体地球仪，兼具几何直觉与动手工艺。是数学、艺术与教育的有趣交汇。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://foldingglobes.com/globes/sun.png' data-full='https://foldingglobes.com/globes/sun.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49084324' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49084324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unattributed.cc/note-taking-and-personal-knowledge-management' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Note-Taking and Personal Knowledge Management</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>笔记与个人知识管理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49084324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49084324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Note-taking is more than just collecting words; it&#x27;s about personal knowledge management. The piece outlines practical methods for organizing notes, linking ideas, and designing retrieval-friendly systems to boost learning and creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>好的笔记不仅是记录信息，更是管理知识的体系。本文提供将笔记分解为可复用的原子笔记、建立双向链接与定期回顾的做法，帮助提高检索与创新效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.snap.as/91youjmK.jpeg' data-full='https://i.snap.as/91youjmK.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49020219' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49020219' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/07/i-wanted-a-clock-that-never-needed-setting-things-escalated/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I wanted a clock that never needed setting. Things escalated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我想要一个永不需要校准的时钟，结果越弄越离谱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49020219'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A self-sufficient clock project started with a goal to avoid manual setting, but the implementation snowballed into a broader exploration of timekeeping, calibration, and robustness. The result offers lessons on building resilient, autonomous devices and the tradeoffs involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最初目标是做出一个永远不需要手动调时钟的装置，结果项目逐步扩展，涉及时间同步、校准与鲁棒性的设计取舍。对构建自主、容错设备的经验教训值得关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/JT3A6491-1152x648-1784649433.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/JT3A6491-1152x648-1784649433.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49113599' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49113599' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ctgt.ai/research/distillation-censorship-transfer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Distilling DeepSeek into GPT-OSS doesn&#x27;t transfer censorship. Try it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：将 DeepSeek 精炼成 GPT-OSS 不带审查的版本，试试看</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113599'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Distilling DeepSeek into an open-source GPT-OSS appears not to transfer censorship, preserving a more open behavior in the recipient model. The result offers a peek at whether censorship constraints travel through model distillation and how open-source variants may differ.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 DeepSeek 精炼为开源 GPT-OSS 的版本似乎不会带入原模型的审查机制，保留更自由的输出行为。该结果揭示了蒸馏过程中审查约束是否会随模型传递，以及开源实现的潜在差异。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67c5c67a4c758597bd28fafd/6a69e158f9fd4e8b99da4607_OG%20Card-selection.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/67c5c67a4c758597bd28fafd/6a69e158f9fd4e8b99da4607_OG%20Card-selection.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49130726' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49130726' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sundry.jerryorr.com/2026/07/31/development-pipeline-is-a-production-system' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The development pipeline is a production system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开发流水线就是生产系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49130726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49130726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49130726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The idea that the development pipeline is a production system emphasizes treating CI/CD, feature flags, and telemetry with manufacturing-level discipline. It argues for strong observability and reliable deployment processes to prevent production-like failures in software releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将开发流水线视为生产系统，强调应对 CI/CD、功能开关和可观测性保持与制造业同等的管理纪律。主张建立可靠的部署流程和可观测性，避免软件发布时出现仿佛生产环境的故障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sundry.jerryorr.com/images/mastodon-icon-black.svg' data-full='https://sundry.jerryorr.com/images/mastodon-icon-black.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49106384' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49106384' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://noyb.eu/en/1741-informed-consents-one-click-gdpr-complaint-against-dictcc-filed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1,741 &quot;informed&quot; consents with one click? GDPR complaint filed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1741个知情同意，一键完成？就GDPR投诉已提交</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49106384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49106384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49106384'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy group NOYB filed a GDPR complaint alleging a consent mechanism enables 1,741 &#x27;informed&#x27; consents with a single click, misrepresenting user consent. The complaint highlights systemic issues with how some services obtain consent for data processing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>隐私倡议团体NOYB就某些服务的一键同意机制提出GDPR投诉，称可用一次点击完成1741项‘知情同意’，误导并不符合同意原则。投诉聚焦数据处理同意的系统性问题，以及对用户权益的潜在侵害。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://noyb.eu/sites/default/files/styles/facebook/public/2026-07/header_dictcc.png?h=c9a3a702&amp;itok=bvl4l6Lx' data-full='https://noyb.eu/sites/default/files/styles/facebook/public/2026-07/header_dictcc.png?h=c9a3a702&amp;itok=bvl4l6Lx' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49120120' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49120120' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cn0nqv05g0do' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Danube&#x27;s record low levels force shutdown of Hungary&#x27;s only nuclear plant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多瑙河纪录低位迫使匈牙利唯一核电厂停运</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49120120'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Record-low Danube water levels forced Hungary to shut down its only nuclear plant for safety, highlighting climate variability risks to critical energy infrastructure and cross-border energy implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多瑙河水位创纪录低点，迫使匈牙利出于安全考虑停运其唯一核电厂，凸显气候波动对关键能源基础设施的风险及跨境能源影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究报告展示了沃尔沃/依车车队平台如何被利用来获取对大量用户与车辆的广泛访问。此披露强调在互联车辆生态中加强 API 安全、认证与供应链风险管理的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此事后分析从根本原因到修复过程梳理了内核健全性漏洞 #14576，阐述其对内存安全的影响及修复方案，强调健壮工具和子系统边界在内核可靠性中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maurycyz.com/misc/tungsten/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Increasing the lifespan of a bulb makes it worse in every other way</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>延长灯泡寿命在其他方面反而更糟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49121849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49121849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49121849'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how extending the lifespan of a bulb can have unintended downsides, such as reduced efficiency, higher material use, or environmental impact, especially for tungsten-based bulbs. It argues for balanced optimization that weighs durability against energy use and lifecycle concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨延长灯泡寿命可能带来的副作用，如效率下降、材料成本增加或环境影响，特别是针对钨丝灯泡。强调在追求耐用性的同时，也要兼顾能效与生命周期问题。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Red Bull-funded research reportedly influenced energy-drink policy, raising concerns about conflicts of interest shaping regulation. The piece calls for greater transparency and independent studies to curb industry-influenced policymaking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称红牛资助的研究曾影响能量饮料相关政策，引发对利益冲突如何塑造监管的担忧。文章呼吁提高透明度，推动更独立的研究以减少行业影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1086489/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arch Linux disables AUR package adoption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Arch Linux 禁用 AUR 包认领</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 121</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux has disabled the AUR&#x27;s package adoption feature, preventing users from claiming ownership of orphaned packages. This change affects maintainers and could impact updates and stability of some AUR software until new maintainers step up.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Arch Linux 已禁用 AUR 的包认领功能，用户无法再接管孤儿包的维护。此变动可能影响部分 AUR 软件的更新与稳定性，直到出现新的维护者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49073942'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to building self-contained, portable Python distributions that bundle the interpreter and dependencies, letting you ship Python apps without system Python. This approach improves reproducibility and deployment simplicity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍自包含、高度便携的 Python 发行版，打包解释器与依赖，让应用不依赖系统 Python 即可运行。此做法提升了再现性与部署的简便性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49122994' data-tags='Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49122994' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/finance/investing/situational-awareness-down-67-in-july-in-ai-stock-rout-cd19901f' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Situational Awareness down 67% in July in AI stock rout</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 股市动荡导致投资者情境感知下降 67%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122994' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49122994'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>July’s AI-stock rout coincided with a sharp drop in situational awareness by about 67%, suggesting traders struggled to interpret fast-moving AI news and valuations. The note highlights how hype cycles can erode risk judgment in tech markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>七月 AI 股票下跌之际，情境感知约下降 67%，表明交易员难以迅速解读快速变化的 AI 新闻与估值。该现象凸显热点周期对科技市场风险判断的负面影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Self-hosting Kimi K3 demonstrates a clear cost-performance trade-off: roughly 20% higher hardware expenditure yields about 20% better task resolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自托管 Kimi K3 展示了明确的成本-性能权衡：硬件投入约高出20%，任务解决能力提升约20%。文章探讨了对私有数据中心、对时延敏感型工作负载以及总拥有成本的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.riscosopen.org/news/articles/2026/06/20/twenty-years-of-risc-os-open' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Twenty Years of RISC OS Open</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RISC OS Open 二十周年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49143967'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two decades after RISC OS Open began, the project has grown through community contributions, new ports, and ongoing maintenance of a historic operating system. The anniversary underscores how open governance can sustain niche platforms on modern hardware and keep a loyal user base engaged.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自 RISC OS Open 推出以来已走过二十年，社区贡献、新移植与持续维护让这套历史操作系统在现代硬件上仍活跃。周年庆凸显开源治理如何支撑小众平台长期发展与社区活跃度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Maxwell Conjecture Is False (GPT 5.6 Sol)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>麦克斯韦猜想被证明错误（GPT-5.6 求解）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49121868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49121868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49121868'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇新的 arXiv 论文声称麦克斯韦猜想是错误的，可能使用 AI 辅助方法（GPT-5.6）得到求解。这项工作引发对 AI 辅助数学证明的严谨性与可重复性问题的讨论，凸显了 AI 在前沿数学中的潜力与挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>7000 年前狩猎采集者将鱼引入山地湖泊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49063983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49063983'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archaeologists report that hunter-gatherers introduced fish to a remote mountain lake about 7,000 years ago, showing early experimentation with freshwater fisheries. The finding sheds light on long-distance mobility, resource diversification, and the roots of sedentary economies in highland regions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>考古学家发现约 7000 年前的狩猎采集者已将鱼带入偏远山地湖泊，显示早期淡水渔业尝试。此发现揭示了远程迁徙、资源多样化，以及高地地区定居经济的起源。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Norvig&#x27;s 1998 piece argues that becoming fluent in programming takes about a decade of deliberate practice, not quick tutorials. It emphasizes solving real problems, building projects, and steadily deepening fundamentals over many years.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49143001'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ars Notoria is a medieval manuscript about memory and rapid knowledge acquisition, contrasted with modern AI promises of instant expertise. The piece reflects on historical quests for quick learning and cautions against shortcuts to understanding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Ars Notoria》是关于记忆与快速获取知识的中世纪手稿，与现代人工智能的即时知识承诺形成对照。本文反思人类对快速学习的历史追求，并警示对理解的捷径风险。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098466'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study details how a frontier lab&#x27;s agent was compromised, outlining attack vectors, security gaps in model deployment pipelines, and lessons for defending research-scale AI deployments. It emphasizes the need for supply chain hardening and stricter access controls in high-velocity AI labs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>案例研究揭示了一家前沿实验室的代理被入侵的过程，梳理了攻击向量、部署管线中的安全漏洞，以及对研究级 AI 部署的防御教训。强调需加强供应链安全与对高强度 AI 实验室的访问控制。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州数据删除请求（DROP）将于8月1日生效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 67</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49148987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California&#x27;s consumer data deletion requests become legally enforceable, requiring firms to honor DELETE requests and purge data where applicable. It raises operational challenges for data brokers, retention policies, and cross-border data flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州的数据删除请求（DROP）将于8月1日开始强制执行，企业需遵循用户删除数据的权利。此举对数据经纪、数据保留策略与跨境数据流动提出了新的合规挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fred.stlouisfed.org/series/GFDEGDQ188S' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. debt-to-GDP ratio reaches 123%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国债务与 GDP 比率达到 123%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123258'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>U.S. debt-to-GDP has reached 123%, signaling a high debt burden relative to output. The level has implications for fiscal policy, interest rates, and the investment climate in tech and innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国债务与 GDP 比率达到 123%，意味着债务水平相对产出处于高位。此水平将影响财政政策、利率走向以及科技与创新领域的投资环境。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mnot.net/blog/2020/for_the_users' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC 8890 – The Internet is for End Users (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFC 8890：互联网为了最终用户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49109474'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFC 8890 argues that the Internet should be designed with the end user in mind, emphasizing usability, privacy, and accessibility. The post reflects on how network protocols and governance affect practical user experiences and the importance of human-centric design in tech standards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFC 8890 指出互联网设计应以最终用户为中心，强调可用性、隐私与无障碍性。文章讨论网络协议与治理如何影响用户体验，强调在技术标准中优先考虑人本设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mnot.net/personal/mn-round-256.png' data-full='https://mnot.net/personal/mn-round-256.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49094151' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49094151' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://16-bits.org/ft2.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fasttracker II clone in C using SDL 2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 SDL2 的 C 实现的 Fasttracker II 克隆</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49094151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49094151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49094151'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This is a C-based clone of the classic Fasttracker II using SDL2, illustrating how retro audio software can be recreated with modern toolchains. It serves as a case study in faithful reproduction and performance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个用 SDL2 实现的经典 Fasttracker II 的克隆，展示了如何用现代工具链重现怀旧音频软件，并带来实现风格与性能的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://16-bits.org/i/contact.gif' data-full='https://16-bits.org/i/contact.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49087499' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49087499' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/GrieferPig/esp32-s31-linux' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux on ESP32</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 ESP32 上运行 Linux</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49087499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49087499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49087499'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A bold experiment ports a Linux kernel to the ESP32-S31 microcontroller, showing the limits of running a full OS on ultra-low-power hardware. The effort highlights the engineering hurdles—memory constraints, boot, drivers—and what it means for edge computing experiments and hobbyist hardware hacking. Not production-ready, but a milestone in expanding where Linux can run.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在极低功耗硬件上尝试将 Linux 内核移植到 ESP32-S31，展示了完整操作系统在此类设备上的边界与挑战。该工作揭示了记忆体约束、启动与驱动等工程难题，以及这对边缘计算实验和爱好者硬件 Black 盒的意义。虽然尚未达到生产就绪，但标志着 Linux 可运行范围的扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6463c172adc4acd3174bd459903b4efb5d967045cd4e011bfe6e8d90f304b84c/GrieferPig/esp32-s31-linux' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6463c172adc4acd3174bd459903b4efb5d967045cd4e011bfe6e8d90f304b84c/GrieferPig/esp32-s31-linux' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49021007' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49021007' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gpuopen.com/learn/ray-tracing-massive-amounts-animated-geometry/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ray tracing massive amounts of animated geometry using tetrahedral cages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用四面体笼对大量动画几何进行光线追踪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49021007'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPUOpen outlines an approach to ray tracing massive animated geometry using tetrahedral cages, a data-structure technique that accelerates intersection tests and reduces memory bandwidth. The method enables more complex scenes with dynamic geometry while keeping rendering costs in check.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPUOpen 介绍了使用四面体笼进行大规模动画几何的光线追踪，这是一种加速相交测试并降低内存带宽的结构化数据方法。该方法在保持可控渲染成本的同时支持更复杂、动态的几何场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gpuopen.com/images/rt_massive_teaser_gamma_featured.C4yHI1jh.jpg' data-full='https://gpuopen.com/images/rt_massive_teaser_gamma_featured.C4yHI1jh.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49119270' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49119270' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zserge.com/posts/worst-htmx-ever/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s make the worst Htmx</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让我们把 Htmx 弄成最糟糕的用法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49119270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49119270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author playfully experiments with deliberately bad Htmx usage to surface common anti-patterns and UX pitfalls. The takeaway is that careful design and clear progressive enhancement strategies matter for robust web apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者通过故意糟糕的 Htmx 用法，揭示了常见的反模式与用户体验陷阱。核心观点是，谨慎的设计和清晰的渐进增强策略对构建稳健的网页应用至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://zserge.com/logo.png' data-full='https://zserge.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49109026' data-tags='Programming,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49109026' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hillelwayne.com/post/why-dont-people-use-formal-methods/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why don&#x27;t people use formal methods? (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么人们不使用形式化方法？（2019）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49109026'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hillel Wayne explains barriers to formal methods adoption: complexity, workflow misalignment, perceived overhead, and cognitive load. The piece argues for pragmatic pathways, like integrating lightweight checks and education, to gradually bring formal methods into mainstream software engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者指出形式化方法在采用上的障碍：复杂性、工作流不匹配、感知开销及认知负担。文章倡导通过更轻量化的检查、教育与渐进式集成等务实路径，让形式化方法逐步进入主流软件工程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49060363' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49060363' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letoverlambda.com/textmode.cl/guest/chap8.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lisp moving Forth moving Lisp</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lisp 的演化：在 Forth 与 Lisp 之间的迁移</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49060363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49060363'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece on how Lisp, Forth, and related languages influenced each other, exploring macro systems, syntax, and extensibility. It argues for simplicity and expressiveness as enduring design goals in language evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文回顾 Lisp、Forth 与相关语言如何互相影响，探讨宏系统、语法与可扩展性等理念。主张简洁性与表达能力是语言演进中恒久的设计目标。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49096885' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096885' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/07/san-francisco-dont-fall-industry-defense-surveillance-pricing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>San Francisco: Don&#x27;t Fall for Industry Defense of Surveillance Pricing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>旧金山：勿相信行业对监控定价的辩护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096885'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece urges skepticism of pricing models that monetize surveillance, arguing such models undermine privacy and deepen inequities. It calls for policy reforms and transparent data practices in urban services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对以监控为生的定价模式提出质疑，认为此类模式侵蚀隐私并加剧不平等，呼吁在城市服务中实施透明的数据实践和政策改革。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/third_party_tracking_banner.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/third_party_tracking_banner.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49108048' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49108048' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bennuttall.com/blog/2026/07/gpiozero-flow/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gpiozero Flow</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gpiozero Flow：树莓派 GPIO 的流水线式编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49108048'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gpiozero Flow introduces a flow-based interface for GPIO programming, aiming to simplify sequencing hardware actions on Raspberry Pi. It helps users express hardware logic as visual or declarative flows, reducing boilerplate and timing pitfalls for beginners and advanced alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gpiozero Flow 引入一种用于 GPIO 编程的流水线式接口，旨在简化树莓派上硬件动作的顺序控制。它让用户以可视化或声明式的流程来表达硬件逻辑，降低了样板代码和时序难题，适合初学者和进阶用户。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bennuttall.com/blog/2026/07/gpiozero-flow/images/gpiozero-flow.webp' data-full='https://bennuttall.com/blog/2026/07/gpiozero-flow/images/gpiozero-flow.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48997008' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-48997008' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.popsci.com/science/whats-jimothy-raccoon-condition/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jimothy the raccoon has a rare spinal condition. Here&#x27;s what that means</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>名为 Jimothy 的浣熊患有罕见脊髓疾病，这意味着什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48997008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48997008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jimothy the raccoon has a rare spinal condition; experts explain how it affects mobility, nerve function, and daily behavior, and what it means for wildlife rehab and care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>名为 Jimothy 的浣熊患有罕见脊髓疾病，专家解释它如何影响运动能力、神经功能与日常行为，以及对野生动物康复照护的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Jimothy-the-raccoon.jpg?w=1200' data-full='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/Jimothy-the-raccoon.jpg?w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49070073' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49070073' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edconway.substack.com/p/the-glass-famine' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Glass Famine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玻璃饥荒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49070073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49070073' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49070073'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a period of glass shortages affecting the supply chain; analysis of economic and societal impact on tech manufacturing and consumer goods, and lessons for resilient supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析玻璃短缺对供应链的影响，以及对科技制造和消费品的冲击，并提出提升供应链韧性的思路。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49145201' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49145201' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ursulakleguin.com/a-rant-about-technology' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Rant About “Technology” (2005)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对“技术”的抨击（2005）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145201' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145201'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ursula K. Le Guin writes a sharp critique of technology and its social impact, urging people to consider what we gain and lose with progress. The piece challenges techno-optimism and emphasizes human values over technological fetish.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乌尔斯拉·K·勒古恩对科技及其社会影响提出尖锐批评，敦促人们反思以进步之名所带来的得失。她质疑科技乐观主义，强调人文价值应置于技术之上。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5cad42ef90f904e520359371/t/61c1853628a5544251cbdc88/1640072502771/image-asset.jpeg?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/5cad42ef90f904e520359371/t/61c1853628a5544251cbdc88/1640072502771/image-asset.jpeg?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49113913' data-tags='Programming,DevOps,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49113913' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A blog post outlines techniques for scaling queue workloads inside PostgreSQL, including approaches like partitioning, indexing, and efficient worker handling to boost throughput. The guidance helps teams run reliable, DB-backed queues at larger scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博文介绍在 PostgreSQL 内部扩展队列工作负载的技术要点，包括分区、索引以及高效工作器处理等方法，以提升吞吐量。为在数据库支撑的队列系统上实现更大规模运营提供实用思路。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149326'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shitty is a fast, memory-unsafe terminal aimed at squeezing maximum speed, trading safety for performance. It&#x27;s a provocative proof-of-concept that highlights what you can achieve by stripping away safety checks, while cautioning against using it in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shitty 是一个追求极致速度、且不保证内存安全的快速终端。通过牺牲安全性来提升性能，这个概念验证展示了在剥离安全检查后能达到的极端性能，同时提示在生产环境中不要使用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://faculty.lsu.edu/kharms/files/flexner_1939.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Usefulness of Useless Knowledge (1939) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1939：《无用知识的用处》[PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49020842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49020842'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flexner&#x27;s 1939 essay defends the value of pure, curiosity-driven knowledge in science and culture. It argues that what seems &#x27;useless&#x27; today often fuels breakthroughs later, a timely reminder for research funding and long-term thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1939 年的《无用知识的用处》主张纯粹知识与好奇驱动的研究对科学与文化发展至关重要。它提醒我们，当前看似无用的探索往往成为未来突破的催化剂。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>揭开习惯形成的奥秘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49139383'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reviews habit formation research, outlining cues, routines, rewards, and environment shaping behavior, and translates neuroscience into practical guidance for designers and individuals aiming to form or break habits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>综述习惯形成的最新研究，解析线索、例行程序、奖励与环境如何塑造行为，并给产品设计与个人改造带来可落地的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/graham33/nixos-dgx-spark' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: NixOS-DGX-Spark – Nix and NixOS on the DGX Spark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: NixOS-DGX-Spark — 在 DGX Spark 上实现 Nix/NixOS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49146267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49146267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49146267'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project porting Nix/NixOS onto Nvidia DGX Spark shows how reproducible environments can be built for AI workloads on a GPU cluster. It demonstrates declarative configuration managing complex GPU drivers and software stacks across nodes, reducing drift.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个在 DGX Spark 上运行 Nix/NixOS 的实验性工作，旨在为 GPU 集群上的 AI 工作负载提供可重复的环境。它展示了如何通过声明式、可重复的配置来统一管理复杂的 GPU 驱动和软件栈，并在多节点间保持一致性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d15bf24ccbaa797bafe9937722b9b426d1607d890b3b88fc5501044dd828e935/graham33/nixos-dgx-spark' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d15bf24ccbaa797bafe9937722b9b426d1607d890b3b88fc5501044dd828e935/graham33/nixos-dgx-spark' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
