<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/schildep/verified-3d-mesh-intersection' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Formally verified 3D CSG: Trust 93 lines spec, not 1000 lines AI code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：形式化验证的3D CSG：93行规范胜过1000行AI代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49083239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49083239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49083239'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A minimal, formally verified 3D CSG engine uses a 93-line specification, offering a compact, verifiable alternative to large AI-based geometry tooling. It demonstrates how formal methods can deliver correctness with tiny specs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个最小化、经过形式化验证的3D CSG引擎使用93行规范，提供了对大型AI几何工具的紧凑且可验证的替代方案。通过极简规范实现正确性 become 可能的例证。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49110348' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49110348' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cam.ac.uk/research/news/study-reveals-why-dna-damage-from-smoking-and-uv-rays-may-cause-cancer-in-some-people-but-not-others' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why DNA damage from smoking and UV rays cause cancer in some but not others</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何烟草暴露与紫外线造成的 DNA 损伤为何并非对所有人都致癌</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49110348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Study explains why DNA damage from smoking and UV exposure leads to cancer in some people but not others, likely due to genetic factors, DNA repair efficiency, and lifestyle interactions. It highlights the role of individual risk profiles and precision medicine.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究揭示烟草暴露与紫外线造成的DNA损伤为何并非对所有人都致癌，原因在于个体基因差异、DNA 修复效率及生活方式的相互作用，强调个体化风险评估的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cam.ac.uksmoking-ashtray.jpg' data-full='https://www.cam.ac.uksmoking-ashtray.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49113801' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.net/plants/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So you want to use plants to reduce CO₂</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>想用植物来降低二氧化碳排放吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113801'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plants contribute to carbon sequestration but face limits in scale, persistence, and speed; realism matters when evaluating nature-based strategies, which should complement tech-driven solutions in climate plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>植物在碳汇方面确有作用，但在规模、持续性和速度上存在局限。评估自然基解决方案时应保持现实主义，将其作为科技驱动方法的补充。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dynomight.net/img/plants/the_writer.jpg' data-full='https://dynomight.net/img/plants/the_writer.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/1202.4212' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Harmony Explained: Progress Towards a Scientific Theory of Music (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>和声解释：迈向音乐科学理论的进展（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49085280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49085280'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2012 preprint surveys progress toward a formal, testable theory of harmony, blending acoustics, music theory, and information theory. It discusses criteria for a rigorous framework and highlights unresolved questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇2012年的预印本综述了朝着正式、可检验的音乐和声理论迈进的进展，融合声学、音乐理论与信息理论。文中提出建立严格框架的评估标准，并指出尚待解决的关键问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以 ASRock BC-250 为核心的预算 Steam 机器构建方案，兼顾成本、散热与软件选择，在 Linux/SteamOS 上实现相对平衡的游戏体验。为追求低成本的家用游戏硬件提供参考。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49079727' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arsastronomica.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ars Astronomica – English translations of rare Hebrew and Latin astronomy texts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ars Astronomica——罕见希伯来语与拉丁文天文学文本的英文翻译</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49079727'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ars Astronomica curates English translations of rare Hebrew and Latin astronomy texts, opening centuries-old insights to contemporary readers. By making primary sources accessible, the project enriches the history of science and invites fresh comparisons between ancient models and modern understandings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ars Astronomica 收集并提供希伯来文与拉丁文天文学文本的英文译本，将濒危的原著带给现代读者。通过研究原始文献，该项目帮助史料学者比较古代与中世纪的宇宙观与观测方法，增加对科学史的理解。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49135245' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexiglad.github.io/blog/2026/explorative_modeling/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Explorative modeling: Train on the best of K guesses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>探索性建模：在前K个猜测中训练最佳模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49135245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49135245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49135245'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post proposes training on the best of K guesses, selecting the top-performing candidate from multiple predictions to guide learning. This explorative modeling approach can improve sample efficiency and robustness in settings with uncertain models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在前K个候选猜测中选取最佳者来引导训练的做法，属于探索性建模。此方法可能提高样本效率与对不确定模型的鲁棒性，适用于多模型并存的情景。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

It&#x27;s a nostalgic look at early cross-platform enthusiasm that foreshadowed modern open ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 MkLinux 在 Apple Workgroup Server 9150 上的实现，讲述爱好者如何把 PPC Mac 改造成 Linux 盒子、遇到的硬件改装与兼容性挑战。展现早期跨平台热情，以及对如今开放生态的先行影响。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49041256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A memory upgrade to 22 GB on the RTX 2080 Ti enables larger offline models and reduces paging on older hardware. It illustrates how a small hardware tweak can extend the life of GPUs for modern workloads, especially in constrained environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RTX 2080 Ti 的显存升至 22 GB，能让老卡在离线场景下承载更大模型并降低内存换出。此举展示了通过小幅硬件改造延长显卡寿命的可行性，尤其在资源受限的环境中。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49096170' data-tags='Data,DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096170' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fzakaria.com/2026/07/27/the-mean-means-nothing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The mean means nothing: data visualization to debug a latency problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>均值并非万金油：用数据可视化调试延迟问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096170'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that relying on averages hides latency tails and outliers, proposing visualization techniques like CDFs, histograms, and latency distributions to diagnose performance problems. It emphasizes how better visuals can guide rapid pinpointing of bottlenecks across systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张仅看均值容易掩盖尾部延迟和离群值，提出使用 CDF、直方图等延迟分布可视化方法来诊断性能问题，强调更清晰的可视化可以帮助快速定位跨系统的瓶颈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fzakaria.com/assets/images/latency-1-mean.svg' data-full='https://fzakaria.com/assets/images/latency-1-mean.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目从零构建一台基于 6502 的计算机，并以 Minitel 作为终端进行输入输出，展现了复古计算的工艺与软硬件的实际整合。通过接入古老硬件与现代工具，提供了有价值的实践经验和教学意义。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://juliahub.com/blog/frontier-models-physical-ai-evaluation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 vs. Claude Fable 5 for Physical AI, which performs best?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49098388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative evaluation of GPT-5.6 and Claude Fable 5 on physical AI tasks shows how different model families handle embodied or robotic reasoning, perception, and real-time control. The findings underscore that instruction-following capabilities and safety constraints still shape real-world usability in robotics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 GPT-5.6 与 Claude Fable 5 在物理 AI 任务上的对比评测显示，不同模型在具身或机器人推理、感知与实时控制方面的差异。结果强调指令遵循与安全约束仍然影响机器人在现实世界的可用性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49145883' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49145883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(297) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.juni-mp4.com/posts/42/rooting-the-tplink-tl841n-pt1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rooting, firmware analysis and persistent credentials of TP-Link TL-841N</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TP-Link TL-841N 路由器的 Rooting、固件分析与持久凭证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 95</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49145883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49145883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post dissects how to root the TP-Link TL-841N and analyzes its firmware to reveal security weaknesses, including credentials that survive reflash. It highlights how consumer routers can harbor persistent backdoors and why such persistence matters for threat modeling and firmware security practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章详细讲解如何对 TP-Link TL-841N 路由器进行 Rooting，并对固件进行分析，揭示安全漏洞及持续存在的凭证。提示了家用路由器中可能存在的持久后门问题，以及在威胁建模和固件安全实践中的意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49021024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rootless containers reduce risk by avoiding root privileges, improving container isolation across hosts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>无根容器通过避免使用根权限来降低风险，提升跨主机的容器隔离。文中给出在服务端部署中实现无根运行时的实用做法、配置与工具，以及在性能与兼容性上的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49132083' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49132083' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rails/solid_queue/releases/tag/v1.6.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 now supports fiber workers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 现已支持纤程工作者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49132083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49132083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49132083'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 adds fiber-based workers, enabling lightweight concurrency inside the queue system. This improves throughput for asynchronous workloads and reduces the need for OS threads, benefiting Ruby/Rails ecosystems and other event-driven stacks. The update makes writing scalable, non-blocking code simpler.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Solid Queue 1.6.0 增加了基于纤程的工作者，允许在队列系统内部实现轻量级并发。提升异步工作负载的吞吐量，减少对操作系统线程的依赖，对 Ruby/Rails 生态和其他事件驱动框架尤其有利。本次更新让编写可扩展、非阻塞代码更为简单。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a76c8f15f77d023fba1cb46bbf1d97ecb78b623081adc1ccdbcdcae101eea58b/rails/solid_queue/releases/tag/v1.6.0' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a76c8f15f77d023fba1cb46bbf1d97ecb78b623081adc1ccdbcdcae101eea58b/rails/solid_queue/releases/tag/v1.6.0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/deep-sea-vehicles-spot-alien-sharks-deep-beneath-waves-pacific#' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deep-sea vehicles spot &#x27;alien&#x27; sharks deep beneath the waves in the Pacific</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>深海探测器在太平洋深处发现“外星”鲨鱼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49140599' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49140599'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Autonomous deep-sea vehicles captured footage of unusual sharks far below the surface, revealing new biology and behavior at depth. The find underscores the diversity of marine life and the value of undersea robotics for discovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深海探测载具在太平洋深处发现形态独特的鲨鱼，揭示深海生物多样性与行为模式的新线索。凸显无人在场下潜和自主潜水器对科学发现的价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lrb.co.uk/the-paper/v48/n14/emily-wilson/an-uncomplicated-man' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An Uncomplicated Man – A review of The Odyssey, directed by Christopher Nolan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个简单的人——克里斯托弗·诺兰执导的《奥德赛》影评</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49078575'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Uncomplicated Man reviews Nolan&#x27;s Odyssey adaptation, examining how the director translates epic poetry into cinema without overcomplicating character or plot. The review weighs stylistic choices and the film’s drive to modernize a classic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《一个简单的人》评析诺兰执导的《奥德赛》改编，探讨导演如何在不让人物和情节过于复杂的前提下，将史诗转化为电影。评论衡量风格选择与现代化处理对经典的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49073834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49073834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yap 是一个在 macOS 上实现本地离线语音输入的开源项目，无需下载模型，强调隐私与离线运行。用戶可获得比云端转写更具隐私保护的替代方案。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://censys.com/blog/cisa-alert-water-tower-plc-targeting/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CISA Alert: Water Sector PLC Targeting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>水务行业 PLC 攻击警报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49137228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49137228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49137228'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CISA warns of targeted attacks against water facilities using PLCs, highlighting risk to ICS/SCADA environments. It recommends segmentation, timely patching, and heightened monitoring to reduce exposure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CISA 警告对水务设施的 PLC 进行定向攻击，可能危及 ICS/SCADA 环境。建议加强网络分段、及时打补丁并提升监控以降低风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49069033' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49069033' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://abeljansma.nl/2026/07/10/truth-is-not-a-direction.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Truth is not a direction: a Tarski attack on LLM probes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>真理不是一个方向：对 LLM 探针的塔斯基攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49069033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49069033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49069033'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Truth is not a directional metric when probing LLMs, argues a Tarski-inspired critique of current evaluation methods. The piece suggests more robust evaluation criteria that account for semantics and context rather than a single directional truth claim.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为对 LLM 探针的‘真相’评估并不是一个单一方向可衡量的指标，存在本质缺陷。借鉴塔斯基逻辑的观点，批评现有探针方法，并提出考虑语义与上下文的更稳健评估标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://abeljansma.nl/assets/blogPics/TINAD_ourobouros.png' data-full='https://abeljansma.nl/assets/blogPics/TINAD_ourobouros.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49062700' data-tags='Education,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49062700' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gracefulliberty.com/articles/teaching-kids-forth/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teaching Kids Forth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>教孩子学 Forth：Anna Liberty</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49062700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49062700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49062700'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forth is a compact, stack-based language that maps well to how computers process instructions. The piece outlines simple, kid-friendly approaches to teaching Forth, with hands-on exercises that build intuition about programming without heavy syntax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Forth 是一种简洁的堆栈式编程语言，能直观地映射计算机执行过程。文章给出面向儿童的实用教学方法与练习，帮助孩子在不依赖复杂语法的情况下建立编程直觉。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gracefulliberty.com/avatar.png' data-full='https://gracefulliberty.com/avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49150561' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modelrepublic.org/articles/the-reporters-at-this-news-site-are-ai-bots.-openai%E2%80%99s-super-pac-appears-to-be-using-it-to-advance-its-political-agenda' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI&#x27;s super PAC is funding AI-generated news site attacking industry critics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 超级 PAC 资助 AI 生成新闻站点攻击行业批评者</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49150561'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A political-action committee aligned with OpenAI is bankrolling an AI-generated news site that targets critics of the AI industry. The project highlights how AI-generated content is being weaponized in political messaging, prompting questions about transparency, media ethics, and regulatory risk as AI companies shape public opinion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与 OpenAI 相关的超级 PAC 资助了一个 AI 生成的新闻网站，专门攻击 AI 行业的批评者。这一举动揭示了 AI 生成内容在政治舆论中的潜在用途，引发对透明度、媒体伦理及监管风险的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at the origins of USB memory sticks, exploring manufacturing challenges, supply chain constraints, and hardware design decisions in the early era of USB flash drives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 USB 内存条在早期阶段的起源，探讨制造挑战、供应链约束以及硬件设计决策对早期闪存存储的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49123468' data-tags='Security,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49123468' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.marcua.net/2026/07/31/authorize-dont-authenticate.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Authorize, don&#x27;t authenticate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>授权，而非认证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49123468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49123468'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for focusing on authorization over authentication in design and security, arguing that verifying who you are is less important than enforcing what they can do. It advocates for fine-grained, resource-based access controls to scale across services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张在设计与安全中优先授权而非认证，强调对资源的访问控制比逐一验证身份更重要。倡导在服务间实现细粒度、基于资源的访问控制以实现可扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.marcua.net/assets/images/og/authorize-dont-authenticate.png' data-full='https://blog.marcua.net/assets/images/og/authorize-dont-authenticate.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49122655' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49122655' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mattbeton.com/blog/bitnet-6502.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Autoregressive Language Model on the 6502 Processor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>6502 处理器上的自回归语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49122655'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An autoregressive language model running on a 6502 processor showcases surprising AI capability within extreme hardware constraints. The project analyzes the engineering tricks and limits of running such models on retro hardware, highlighting edge-AI feasibility and design tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 6502 处理器上运行的自回归语言模型展示了在极端硬件约束下的 AI 能力。该项目讨论在复古硬件上实现此类模型的工程技巧与极限，凸显边缘 AI 的可行性与设计取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mattbeton.com/img/blog/bitnet-6502/header.png' data-full='https://mattbeton.com/img/blog/bitnet-6502/header.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49098260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen Scribe provides offline, local transcription/dictation on Apple Silicon, enabling privacy-preserving voice-to-text without cloud services. It highlights growing efforts to run AI assistants locally on consumer hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen Scribe 提供在 Apple Silicon 上的离线本地转录/口述，确保隐私不通过云端传输。反映了在消费级硬件上本地运行 AI 助手的日益努力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/YoanWai/agent-manager' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agent-Manager: A Tmux TUI for Running Claude Code, Codex and OpenCode</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49107749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49107749' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49074817' data-tags='Startups,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49074817' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rise-reforming.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Rise Reforming (YC S26) – Turning Waste Gases into Valuable Chemicals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rise Reforming：将废气转化为有价值化学品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49074817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 82</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49074817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49074817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rise Reforming, a YC S26-backed startup, turns waste gases into valuable chemicals, tackling industrial emissions while creating new feedstocks. This approach highlights a growing trend in carbon-negative chemistry and could reshape petrochemical sourcing if scalable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rise Reforming 是 YC S26 支持的初创公司，利用废气转化为可用化学品，努力减少工业排放并创造新的原料来源。此举体现了碳负化学的日益兴起，若实现规模化，或将重塑石化原料的来源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.wixstatic.com/media/4e3194_f51bf439cad241aeb0d95b6e4e3b095d~mv2.png/v1/fit/w_2500,h_1330,al_c/4e3194_f51bf439cad241aeb0d95b6e4e3b095d~mv2.png' data-full='https://static.wixstatic.com/media/4e3194_f51bf439cad241aeb0d95b6e4e3b095d~mv2.png/v1/fit/w_2500,h_1330,al_c/4e3194_f51bf439cad241aeb0d95b6e4e3b095d~mv2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(318) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleantechnica.com/2026/08/02/europe-ev-sales-report-bevs-jump-50-reach-26-market-share/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Europe EV Sales BEVs Jump 50% &amp; Reach 26% Market Share</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49147450'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe saw BEV sales up sharply, capturing around a quarter of the market as BEVs rise 50%. The growth signals shifting consumer demand and policy alignment, with implications for grid planning and automotive supply.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲电动车销量显著上升，BEV 市场份额达到约26%，同比增长约50%。这显示消费者需求、政策取向与电动车供应链均在加速转型，对电网与汽车产业有深远影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article outlines approaches to disrupt and defend against supply-chain attacks targeting npm packages and GitHub Actions workflows, including verification, dependency auditing, and provenance tracking. </div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49118696'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bond markets are not embracing the Fed chair’s policy trajectory, with yields signaling a more cautious outlook than the central bank’s messaging. Investors price in slower rate moves and potential pivots, raising risks for risk assets if the gap widens.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>债市对美联储主席的政策前景并不买账，收益率显示投资者对路径更谨慎。市场定价的利率路径可能与央行沟通存在差距，影响风险资产。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://justinflick.com/2026/07/27/llm-confidence-scores.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t ask an LLM for a confidence score</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要向LLM请求置信度分数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49077443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49077443' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49077443'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Don&#x27;t ask an LLM for a confidence score—calibration is unreliable and can mislead decision-making. The post argues for alternative uncertainty measures and external verification to avoid overtrusting model outputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不要向大语言模型请求置信度分数——校准问题使得置信度不可靠，可能误导决策。文章建议采用其他不确定性度量和外部验证来避免过度信任模型输出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://justinflick.com/assets/images/generated/llm-confidence-scores-header.jpeg' data-full='https://justinflick.com/assets/images/generated/llm-confidence-scores-header.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49097730' data-tags='Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49097730' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tracewayapp.com/blog/sqlite-vs-duckdb' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Choose DuckDB rather than SQLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DuckDB 优于 SQLite？还是选择 DuckDB？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49097730'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that for analytical workloads, DuckDB offers better performance and SQL capabilities than SQLite, while SQLite remains strong for simple, embedded scenarios. It discusses trade-offs and recommended use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在分析型工作负载下，DuckDB 相较于 SQLite 在性能和 SQL 功能上更具优势；而 SQLite 仍适用于简单、嵌入式场景。讨论了取舍与推荐的使用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://tracewayapp.com/images/logo.png' data-full='https://tracewayapp.com/images/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49118933' data-tags='AI,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49118933' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://greyswansignals.com/?theme=dark' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The AI trade now runs on borrowed money, and the lenders are repricing it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 投资如今靠借贷支撑，放贷方正在重新定价</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49118933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49118933'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI startups increasingly rely on debt and non-dilutive funding; lenders are re-pricing risk as rates rise and market sentiment shifts. This tightening funding environment could slow AI deployment and growth unless profitability improves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 初创公司日益依赖债务与非股权资金；利率走高和市场情绪转变使放贷方重新定价风险。资金紧缩可能放慢 AI 的部署和增长，除非盈利能力改善。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://greyswansignals.com/og-swan-1200x630.png' data-full='https://greyswansignals.com/og-swan-1200x630.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://broadbandbreakfast.com/how-to-fight-back-against-fraudulent-robocalls/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anti-fraud tools can&#x27;t keep pace with robocall scammers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>防诈骗工具难以跟上机器人电话骗子的步伐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49122882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49122882'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of current anti-fraud tools notes that robocall scammers adapt faster than defenses, calling for stronger authentication, better reporting, and user education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对当前防欺诈工具在对抗机器人电话诈骗方面的有效性提出批评，指出骗子的适应性强于防御措施，呼吁加强认证、报告与用户教育。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49139711' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49139711' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc10015.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC 10015: Deprecating Obsolete Key Exchange Methods in TLS 1.2 and DTLS 1.2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFC 10015：在 TLS 1.2/DTLS 1.2 中弃用过时的密钥交换方法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49139711'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFC 10015 recommends deprecating older TLS/DTLS key exchange methods to improve security posture as older ciphers and DH groups fall out of favor. It provides migration guidance for administrators to ensure safer handshakes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFC 10015 建议在 TLS 1.2/DTLS 1.2 中弃用过时的密钥交换方法，以提升安全性。文中给出服务器和客户端迁移的具体步骤与注意事项。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49065498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49065498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU regulators fined Google $1.02B for favoring its own services, underscoring ongoing antitrust scrutiny of big tech in Europe. The case illustrates the tension between platform ecosystems and fair competition, with potential ripple effects for developers and advertisers. It signals continued appetite for regulation in digital markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟因谷歌偏袒自家服务被罚 10.2 亿美元，体现对大型科技公司持续的反垄断监管。案例揭示平台生态优势与公平竞争之间的博弈，以及对开发者和广告商的潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本指南记录了对 ESP32-C3 SuperMini 模组天线的实际修改方法，以提升覆盖范围与信号质量，并给出测量数据与注意事项，适合爱好者与嵌入式开发者。</p>

The piece demonstrates that while dispersion and interference remain challenges, clever engineering can still yield meaningful improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 2019 年的长距离 2.4 GHz 无线网络实验，强调天线设计、功率边界与实际覆盖的取舍。尽管干扰仍是挑战，但巧妙的工程仍能带来显著的性能提升。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/this-viral-data-center-sounds-like-satans-buzzsaw-2000791122' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Destroying a Community with a Gigantic &quot;Clogged Vacuum Cleaner&quot;</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49068760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49068760'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Media coverage highlights a data center’s massive cooling/noise footprint harming nearby communities, prompting questions about environmental costs of hyperscale infrastructure. The piece urges more considerate design and governance of critical tech infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于大型数据中心的冷却系统造成的噪音与能耗对邻里社区的影响的报道，凸显超大规模基础设施的社会与环境成本。呼吁在设计与治理上更具包容性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49081851' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49081851' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nyctography' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nyctography: A substituton cypher by Lewis Carroll</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nyctography：刘易斯·卡罗尔的夜间替代密码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49081851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49081851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49081851'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nyctography is Lewis Carroll’s night-writing cipher, a playful substitution system designed for secret notes by lamplight. The piece explains how the system worked and its place in Carroll’s linguistic experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nyctography 是刘易斯·卡罗尔在夜间写秘密笔记时使用的替代密码，体现了其语言游戏中的趣味与实验。文中介绍了其工作原理及在卡罗尔创作中的地位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b1/Nyctograph_reconstruction.jpg' data-full='https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/b/b1/Nyctograph_reconstruction.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.pytorch.org/devlogs/compiler/2026-07-25-pytorch-a-reference-language/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PyTorch: A Reference Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PyTorch：一种参考语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49079454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PyTorch: A Reference Language argues for treating PyTorch as a canonical ML &#x27;language&#x27; to experiment with compiler ideas and cross-framework optimizations. The concept could influence how researchers compare models and how tooling evolves around dynamic graphs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PyTorch：一种参考语言提出把 PyTorch 打造成 ML 的‘参考语言’，以便在编译器层面进行探索和跨框架优化。此想法可能影响研究者比较框架的方式，以及对动态图编译的工具发展。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49095628'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpecForge provides tooling to author, verify, and manage formal specifications, bridging between informal requirements and machine-checkable proofs. The project aims to improve correctness in software design by integrating formal methods into workflows.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prized.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Prized (YC S26) – Let non-engineer staff build secure internal tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Prized（YC S26）让非工程师员工构建安全的内部工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49109721'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Prized provides a platform enabling non-engineers to build internal tools with built-in security controls. The system emphasizes governance, access policies, and auditable workflows to reduce reliance on IT teams. For startups and larger orgs, it hints at a shift toward democratized tooling without sacrificing security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Prized 提供了一个平台，让非工程师也能在受控环境下构建内部工具，并内置安全治理与审核机制。通过明确定义的访问策略与工作流，降低对 IT 部门的依赖。此举预示着在不牺牲安全性的前提下，企业内部工具的民主化趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中解释了为何 Book Corners 无法将贡献同步回 OpenStreetMap，原因包括许可、来源追踪和双向同步的技术挑战。该讨论揭示了开放地图工作流中的互操作性难题。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49079490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neutrino-8B refers to a model around boron-8 solar neutrinos, a key probe of solar physics and neutrino oscillations. The work outlines predictions or simulations that could inform experimental analyses and interpretations in particle astrophysics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中微子-8B 指涉一类与 boron-8 脱落相关的中微子模型，聚焦太阳中微子产生机制与振荡现象。该工作提供对中微子与太阳物理的预测或模拟，对实验分析有潜在的参考价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.codingconfessions.com/p/how-unix-spell-ran-in-64kb-ram' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Unix spell ran in 64 kB of RAM (2025)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49066750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49066750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49066750'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retro computing exploration shows how Unix ran in 64 KB of RAM, revealing memory-saving tricks, compact kernel design, and clever use of limited resources. The piece underscores how early OSes achieved remarkable functionality under severe constraints. It’s a reminder of engineering ingenuity, not just raw horsepower.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇回顾性文章讲述 Unix 如何在仅 64KB RAM 的条件下运行，展示了在资源极度受限时的内存管理与紧凑内核设计。凸显早期操作系统在极简约中的工程巧思。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nsf.gov/news/nsf-partners-universities-industry-pilot-initiative-four' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NSF pilots 4-year PhDs with industry research placements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NSF试点四年制博士项目：将产业研究岗位嵌入学位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49105642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49105642' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49105642'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NSF is piloting a program that embeds industry research placements into a four-year PhD track, aiming to accelerate tech transfer and workforce readiness. It blends academia with real-world applied projects, potentially changing how PhD education and funding are structured. Expect more co-op style research and stronger industry partnerships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NSF正在试点一种将产业研究岗位嵌入四年制博士教育的计划，旨在提升研究与产业之间的衔接与人才培养效率。该方案把学术研究与现实应用结合，可能改变博士教育和资助的结构，推动更紧密的产学研合作。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49101529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49101529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49101529'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical look at how intelligence is commodified via circular AI deals, weighing benefits like interoperability against risks of over‑reliance, data leakage, and market distortions. It argues for governance and transparency in AI commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨人工智能商品化带来的机遇与挑战：互操作性等好处与对过度依赖、数据外泄、市场失衡的风险并存。主张在 AI 商业化中加强治理和透明度。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49067043'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shay locomotives were heavy-duty geared steam engines designed for mountain grades in the American rail network. Their mechanical approach influenced later locomotive design and industrial engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shay 蒸汽机车是为美国山区线路设计的重型齿轮蒸汽机车，其机械结构对后续机车设计和工业工程产生了影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49148128' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linuxiac.com/eu-age-verification-project-mandates-hardware-bound-attestation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU Age Verification Project Mandates Hardware-Bound Attestation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟年龄验证计划强制硬件绑定的认证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148128' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49148128'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU&#x27;s age-verification initiative would require hardware-bound attestation to verify user age, raising privacy, accessibility, and security questions. The move could push TPM- or secure enclave-based checks into compliance workflows and affect device manufacturers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的年龄验证计划可能要求硬件绑定的证明来核验年龄，引发隐私、可访问性和安全性方面的讨论。此举或将把信任链和设备特征纳入合规流程，并影响厂商设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://linuxiac.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/eu-digital-identity.jpg' data-full='https://linuxiac.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/eu-digital-identity.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful Show HN post about a tiny project that makes a framework&#x27;s runtime or output audible as a creaky door sound, using audio as a status signal. It riffs on developer UX and demonstrates how small feedback loops can feel surprisingly expressive.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个极具趣味性的 Show HN 项目：让 Framework 12 产生嘎吱作响的门声，用声音作为状态信号。通过这个小实验，展示了以声音提升开发者体验的可能性。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49109759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elena is a library designed for building Progressive Web Components, offering a concise API for composing custom elements with templating and reactive state. It aims to streamline component-based web apps without heavy boilerplate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Elena 提供简洁 API 的库，用于构建渐进式网页组件，支持模板化和响应式状态管理，目标是用更少的样板代码实现组件化的网页应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(370) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thedecisionlab.com/biases/the-illusion-of-explanatory-depth' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why do we think we understand the world more than we actually do?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么我们自以为比实际更懂这个世界？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49079175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49079175'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Humans overestimate how well we understand complex phenomena, a bias known as the illusion of explanatory depth.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on guide to building a fast lock-free queue in modern C++, addressing memory management, ABA problems, and performance trade-offs. It offers a template for high-throughput concurrent data structures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实战讲解在现代 C++ 中从零构建高性能无锁队列，涵盖内存管理、ABA 问题与性能权衡，提供高吞吐并发数据结构的模板。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49120230' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gaming/2026/07/the-disc-is-not-the-game-physical-releases-increasingly-require-extra-downloads/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Just because a game is on disc doesn&#x27;t mean it will work in the future</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>就算游戏有光盘也不代表将来还能玩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49120230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49120230'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physical disc releases increasingly require extra downloads or server checks, meaning a disc is not a guarantee of future playability. The piece calls for better archiving, licensing clarity, and ongoing publisher support to preserve classics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实体光盘越来越多地需要额外下载或服务器校验，这意味着光盘并不能保证未来仍可玩。文章呼吁改进存档、许可透明度，以及发行方的持续支持以保存经典作品。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/discburn-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/discburn-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49062120' data-tags='Space,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49062120' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/space-debris-atmosphere-burn-up' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plasma Tunnels Reveal How Dying Satellites Fall to Earth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>等离子体隧道揭示垂死卫星如何坠落地球</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49062120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49062120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49062120'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers model how dying satellites interact with the upper atmosphere during reentry, revealing how plasma effects shape fragmentation and burn-up. The findings help refine debris risk assessments and inform future satellite design for safer end-of-life disposal.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者模拟垂死卫星在再入大气层时与等离子体的相互作用，揭示等离子体效应如何影响卫星碎裂与烧毁过程。这些发现有助于改进碎片风险评估，并为卫星安全退役设计提供参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67521175&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C156%2C0%2C94' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67521175&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C156%2C0%2C94' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/07/27/nx-s1-5905360/kalshi-wisconsin-election-betting-prediction-markets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kalshi attacks a Wisconsin law banning election bets as &#x27;voter suppression&#x27;</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097209' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49097209'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kalshi challenges Wisconsin&#x27;s election-betting ban as suppressing voters, arguing the law stifles financial markets for political events. The case highlights regulatory uncertainty around prediction markets and their use in political processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kalshi 将威斯康星州禁止选举赌注的法律视为压制选民的行为，认为该法律限制了政治事件的金融市场。案件凸显了预测市场在政治过程中的监管不确定性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49088098' data-tags='Programming,Web,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49088098' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ccsim.fly.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Simulating TCP loss and congestion in browser using Go/WASM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在浏览器中用 Go/WASM 仿真 TCP 丢包与拥塞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49088098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49088098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49088098'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based TCP loss and congestion simulator built with Go and WASM enables experiments without external tools, useful for education and rapid prototyping of transport behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个在浏览器中运行的 TCP 丢包与拥塞仿真器，使用 Go 和 WASM 构建，便于在不依赖外部工具的情况下进行教育与快速原型开发。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49128323' data-tags='Hardware,Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49128323' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Demystifying DRAM Read Disturbance: RowHammer and RowPress Phenomena</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>破解 DRAM 读取干扰：RowHammer 与 RowPress</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49128323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49128323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49128323'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how DRAM can suffer read disturbance, notably RowHammer and RowPress phenomena, where repeated reads can flip nearby bits. It surveys susceptibility across memory types, historical mitigations (ECC, target refresh), and implications for security and reliability in data centers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解释了 DRAM 读取干扰现象，特别是 RowHammer 与 RowPress，重复读取可能导致相邻位翻转。它回顾了对不同内存类型的脆弱性、历史上的缓解措施（ECC、定点刷新）及对数据中心安全与可靠性的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49108556' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49108556' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-07-29/chatgpt-roblox-to-fall-under-strictest-eu-rules-for-platforms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT, Roblox to Fall Under Strictest EU Rules for Platforms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 与 Roblox 将纳入欧洲最严格的平台规则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108556' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49108556'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU rules for platforms are tightening to include AI-powered chatbots and games; obligations around moderation, transparency, and safety. This signals heavier regulatory compliance burdens for consumer tech platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲对平台的监管进一步收紧，ChatGPT 和 Roblox 等将纳入最严格的平台规则，涉及内容审核、透明度与安全等方面的义务，加剧合规成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49108542' data-tags='Design,Web'>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49108542' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://steelmanlabs.com/blog/computer-use-is-far-from-solved' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can&#x27;t solve computer use by ignoring the interface</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你不能通过忽略界面来解决计算机使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49108542' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49108542'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that user experience and interface design are central to solving broader issues with computer use; cognitive load, accessibility, and learnability require better interfaces rather than solely focusing on backend improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张要解决人们使用计算机的问题，必须从界面设计着手，降低认知负荷、提升可访问性与可学性，而非仅优化后端。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49130295' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49130295' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://piegames.de/dumps/a-week-in-matrix/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A week in Matrix</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Matrix 一周观察</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49130295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 57</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49130295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49130295'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a snapshot of recent activity in the Matrix ecosystem, from client updates to federation tweaks and community projects. It underscores Matrix as a live case study in open, federated communication and data sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Matrix 一周观察汇总了最近一周在去中心化通信协议中的动态，如客户端更新、联邦改进和社区项目的进展。Matrix 作为开放式、跨机构通信的实践案例，展示了去中心化协作的机遇与挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kaisel 引入了路由即值的理念，作为 Flutter 的 Dart 3 原生路由器的一部分，提供类型安全、可在编译时校验的导航。该设计旨在提升大型应用的可维护性与性能，减少样板代码和路由错误。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49135546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pgtestdb clones fresh test environments by leveraging template databases, dramatically speeding up test setups and CI runs. The approach maintains isolation while sharing storage, simplifying repeatable testing workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pgtestdb 通过模板数据库克隆创建新的测试环境，大幅加快测试搭建与持续集成的速度。该方法在共享存储条件下仍能保持隔离，简化可重复的测试流程。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.n16f.net/blog/concurrency-interactivity-mutability-choose-two/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Concurrency, interactivity, mutability, choose two</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>并发、交互、可变性：三选二</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49065874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49065874' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49065874'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues you can&#x27;t have all three properties at once; choose two and plan architecture around that choice. The post gives practical patterns for balancing responsiveness, safe concurrent updates, and mutability, with real-world examples from UI frameworks and data streams. It highlights that the decision often drives language and library choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出在三者之间做出选择的设计原则：并发、交互和可变性不可兼得全貌。给出在 UI、数据流等场景中的实用策略与模式，帮助在保持响应性与并发安全之间取得平衡。强调这类选择往往驱动语言与库的选型。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lets Seal 提供免费且自托管的文档签名方案，借鉴 Let’s Encrypt 的信任模型，简化私有签名工作流，避免对外部服务的依赖。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49049992' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49049992' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(410) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hyperbo.la/w/winding-down-artichoke-ruby/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Winding Down Artichoke Ruby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Artichoke Ruby 项目逐步停止</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49049992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49049992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49049992'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artichoke, a Ruby interpreter implemented in Rust, is winding down, signaling a shift in the Ruby ecosystem’s runtime strategies. The move may reflect broader trends toward alternative runtimes and language interop, urging developers to evaluate compatibility and migration paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 Rust 实现的 Ruby 解释器 Artichoke 将逐步停止开发，标志着 Ruby 生态在运行时策略上的转变。此举凸显向替代运行时与语言互操作性的趋势，促使开发者评估兼容性与迁移路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hyperbo.la/social/w/winding-down-artichoke-ruby.png' data-full='https://hyperbo.la/social/w/winding-down-artichoke-ruby.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/saber-toothed-cats-became-inbred-and-struggled-move-they-went-extinct' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Saber-toothed cats became inbred–and struggled to move–before they went extinct</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>剑齿虎在灭绝前因近亲繁殖而运动能力下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49116074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49116074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49116074'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Saber-toothed cats likely hit extinction after inbreeding reduced mobility and adaptability. The finding illustrates how small populations and genetic bottlenecks can doom even formidable predators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>剑齿虎在灭绝前因近亲繁殖而逐渐失去行动能力，可能是其灭绝的原因之一。这项研究强调了小群体基因多样性下降对大型捕食者生存的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/n67094/sdl_gp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SDL_GPU minimal, single-header, high-performance 2D graphics painting library</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49110655' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49110655'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SDL_GPU is a minimal, single-header library for high-performance 2D graphics painting with no dependencies. It aims to be easy to integrate into games and tools that need fast rendering without heavyweight graphics stacks. It&#x27;s a practical option when you want portability and speed with minimal setup.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SDL_GPU 是一个极简且单头文件的高性能二维绘图库，无需外部依赖。它面向需要快速渲染的游戏和工具，减少了图形栈的复杂性，便于快速集成并保持跨平台性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://purplesyringa.moe/blog/log-is-non-monotonic-in-php-and-lua/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Log is non-monotonic in PHP and Lua</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 PHP 与 Lua 中，对数函数并非单调</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49021756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49021756'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Floating-point and language quirks can make logarithm results non-monotonic under certain inputs, undermining assumptions in numeric code. The post analyzes examples in PHP and Lua and offers practical workarounds to preserve monotonicity or avoid relying on it for correctness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在某些输入下，PHP 与 Lua 的对数运算可能出现非单调现象，暴露数值计算中的潜在问题。文中给出示例并提供实用对策，帮助开发者在需要单调性的场景中避免误用或进行稳健处理。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/johnsonjh/cpm386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CP/M-386 – CP/M for 386 protected mode, derived from CP/M‑68K</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149898'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CP/M-386 brings CP/M to 386-class hardware in protected mode, derived from CP/M-68K. It serves as a nostalgic platform for retro computing and a testbed for understanding legacy OS concepts on modern CPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CP/M-386 将经典 CP/M 移植到 386 架构的保护模式，源自 CP/M‑68K。它为复古计算爱好者提供一个怀旧平台，同时帮助学习在现代 CPU 上实现遗留操作系统的思路。</p>
