</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49049724'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Space-filling curves offer locality-preserving maps from multi-dimensional data to one dimension, with several combinatorial applications. The work surveys uses in indexing, partitioning, and graph algorithms, showcasing practical algorithmic benefits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>空间填充曲线将多维数据映射到一维，同时保持局部性，具备多种组合应用。文章介绍在索引、划分和图算法中的用途，展示算法层面的实际收益。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49093232' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49093232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(430) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://transformer-transformer.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Transformer Transformer: A Unified Model for Motion-Conditioned Robot Co-Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Transformer Transformer：面向运动条件的机器人协同设计的统一模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49093232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Transformer-based architecture that jointly models robot morphology and motion under given tasks. This unified model enables end-to-end co-design, potentially shortening iteration cycles between hardware and control policies. It demonstrates how sequence models can reason about physical form alongside behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于 Transformer 的统一模型，能够在给定任务下同时建模机器人形态与运动，支持端到端的硬件-控制协同设计。此方法有望缩短机器人硬件与控制策略之间的迭代周期，并展示序列模型在物理形状与行为推理方面的潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://transformer-transformer.github.io/static/images/arxiv.svg' data-full='https://transformer-transformer.github.io/static/images/arxiv.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://plato.stanford.edu/entries/computational-mind/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Computational Theory of Mind (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>心智的计算理论（2015）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49149125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49149125'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of the computational theory of mind, treating mental states as computations manipulating representations. The entry surveys arguments for and against equating cognition with formal symbol manipulation and discusses implications for AI, cognitive science, and neuroscience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇斯坦福哲学百科全书条目概述了心智的计算理论，将心理状态视为对表征进行计算的过程；讨论将认知等同于形式符号操作的论点及其反对意见，以及对人工智能、认知科学与神经科学的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://plato.stanford.edu/symbols/sep-man-red.png' data-full='https://plato.stanford.edu/symbols/sep-man-red.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(437) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archive.org/details/mancomputerbyjoh0000john' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man and the Computer by John G. Kemeny (1972 book by the co-creator of BASIC)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《人和计算机》：BASIC共同创始人凯梅尼的1972年著作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49104140'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 1972 classic argues for human-centered computing, balancing human cognition with machine power. It provides early insights into user interfaces, programming, and the social implications of personal computing—relevant to designers and developers building human-friendly software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本1972年的经典著作主张以人为本的计算，强调人类认知与机器能力的平衡。书中早期探讨了用户界面、编程以及个人计算对社会的影响，对面向人机交互的软件设计者尤具启发。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49061213' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(440) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.coptic-magic.phil.uni-wuerzburg.de/index.php/2026/01/16/angels-in-coptic-magic-i-introduction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Angels in Coptic Magic I: Introduction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科普特魔法中的天使 I：导论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49061213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49061213' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49061213'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a project examining angels in Coptic magical practice, outlining historical context, sources, and methodology. It questions how angels are invoked, mediated, and integrated into ritual paraphernalia, and what that reveals about late antique religious syncretism. It&#x27;s an anchor for a broader exploration of belief, magic, and community memory in Coptic traditions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍科普特魔法传统中的天使研究，概述历史背景、文本来源与研究方法。探讨天使在仪式中的功能、 mediations 和与埃及与早期基督教信仰的融合。为后续对科普特传统信仰与魔法的深入研究奠定基础。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.coptic-magic.phil.uni-wuerzburg.de/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/N-1126-00-000067-wpu.jpg' data-full='https://www.coptic-magic.phil.uni-wuerzburg.de/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/N-1126-00-000067-wpu.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A review of TinyNES, a micro NES console with an ultra-niche footprint. It covers hardware build, emulation accuracy, game compatibility, and who would enjoy such a focused retro device.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.makonea.com/en-US/blog/programming-languages-are-authoring-tools-for-platforms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Programming Languages Are Authoring Tools for Platforms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编程语言是平台的创作工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49034534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49034534'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Programming Languages Are Authoring Tools for Platforms presents the thesis that languages shape platform ecosystems, not just run on them. It discusses design implications for how languages encode abstractions, tradeoffs, and APIs that steer platform capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论点认为语言不仅是实现工具，也是塑造平台生态的设计杠杆。文章讨论语言如何编码抽象、权衡取舍，以及对 API 的影响，从而推动平台能力的演进。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hex.ooo/library/alicebob.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Alice and Bob After Dinner Speech (1984)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Alice 与 Bob 晚餐后的演讲（1984）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49067162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49067162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49067162'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A witty after-dinner piece on cryptography, presenting the famous &#x27;Alice and Bob&#x27; anecdotes and lessons that shaped cryptography discourse. It remains a charming historical snapshot of early cryptography culture and practical etiquette.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇关于密码学的幽默晚宴演讲，讲述著名的 Alice 与 Bob 的故事及其对密码学的启示，反映早期密码学界的文化与礼仪。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49148159' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49148159' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://seanhelvey.com/tools-and-their-tools/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Mania: From Tulips to Tokens</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 疯狂：从郁金香到代币</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49148159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49148159'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A meditation on hype cycles in AI, tracing parallels from past speculative manias to today’s tokenized tools, and urging skepticism about hype while acknowledging practical toolchains value.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>反思 AI 热潮的周期性，从历史上的投机狂热到如今的工具化 AI，提醒读者在追逐新工具时保持健康的怀疑态度，同时肯定务实的工具链价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://seanhelvey.com/assets/images/seanhelvey/2026/MechanicalTurk.jpg' data-full='https://seanhelvey.com/assets/images/seanhelvey/2026/MechanicalTurk.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49141937' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49141937' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(445) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://osadmins.com/en/ibm-i-os-400-the-database-operating-system/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IBM i (OS/400) the Database Operating System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IBM i（OS/400）：数据库操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49141937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49141937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49141937'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IBM i, formerly OS/400, is known as a database operating system that tightly integrates the DB2 database with the OS, offering high reliability and a single-stack development experience for enterprise workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IBM i（前身为 OS/400）被视为数据库操作系统，将 DB2 数据库与操作系统紧密集成，提供高可靠性与面向企业工作负载的一体化开发体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://osadmins.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/PXL_20260224_222931802-1-scaled.jpg' data-full='http://osadmins.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/PXL_20260224_222931802-1-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49078266' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49078266' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(446) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jacob.gold/posts/residential-proxies-are-a-national-security-threat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Residential Proxies Are a National Security Threat</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>住宅代理构成国家安全威胁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49078266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49078266'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Residential proxies help bypass blocks and mask origins, enabling mass scraping and abuse. The piece argues they raise attribution, privacy, and security challenges, calling for tighter controls and detection tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>住宅代理有助于绕过封锁、隐藏来源，使大规模抓取与滥用成为可能。这带来溯源、隐私和安全方面的挑战，呼吁加强监管与检测手段。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jacob.gold/images/residential-proxies-are-a-national-security-threat.png' data-full='https://jacob.gold/images/residential-proxies-are-a-national-security-threat.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49033522' data-tags='Business,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49033522' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://getdx.com/research/measuring-developer-productivity-with-the-dx-core-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Measuring developer productivity with the DX Core 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 DX Core 4 衡量开发者生产力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49033522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49033522'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DX Core 4 provides a framework to measure developer productivity using multi-dimensional metrics such as output, time to impact, code quality, and collaboration. The approach emphasizes that productivity is not a single metric and aims to give teams actionable data to improve workflows. It signals a shift toward data-informed engineering management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DX Core 4 提供一个衡量开发者生产力的多维度框架，涵盖输出、影响时间、代码质量和协作等指标。方法强调生产力非单一指标，帮助团队通过数据改进工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://getdx.com/img/research/core4/ogimage.png' data-full='https://getdx.com/img/research/core4/ogimage.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49146238' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.archlinux.org/archives/list/aur-general@lists.archlinux.org/message/YPJ3FQYJTJXXY3RUXCYLMHUKHLIUNVFF/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pushes to arch AUR are suspendended right now.</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49146238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49146238' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49146238'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pushes to Arch Linux AUR are suspended, halting new package submissions and updates. This pause affects maintainers and users relying on community contributions for software availability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当前 Arch Linux 的 AUR 提交推送暂停，阻止新包提交和更新。此举将影响维护者和依赖 AUR 的用户，对软件获取产生直接影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49101371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49101371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49101371'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A finding that motion sickness can be alleviated by toggling certain iPhone settings, offering a practical cure inside a phone&#x27;s accessibility options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>发现通过调整 iPhone 的某些设置就能缓解晕动病，为用户提供在手机辅助功能里可直接应用的实用缓解方法。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Valve-Sponsors-RADV-Windows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Valve Sponsors Work Bringing Open-Source RADV Driver to Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Valve 资助将开源 RADV 驱动带到 Windows</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49097591' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49097591'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve funds a project to bring the open-source RADV Vulkan driver to Windows, improving Linux-to-Windows compatibility for AMD GPUs and empowering open-source graphics stacks on Windows. The move hints at broader cross-platform ambitions and driver freedom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 资助将开源 RADV Vulkan 驱动带到 Windows，提升 AMD GPU 在 Windows 上的开源图形栈能力，推动跨平台兼容性与驱动自由。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linguistic research points to a historically richer, perhaps overlooked, period of language diversity, with implications for anthropology, AI linguistics, and historical narrative.</p>

It demonstrates how small utilities can streamline developer experience when using AI coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude-account 提供一个轻量工具，可在不重新登录的情况下切换 Claude Code 账户，提升多账户协作与工作流。展示了小型工具在 AI 编码工具中的实用价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49147978' data-tags='DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49093435' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(461) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.collabora.com/news-and-blog/news-and-events/cracking-windows-open-porting-radv-to-win32.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cracking Windows Open: Porting RADV to Win32</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Win32 上移植 RADV</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49093435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49093435'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RADV, the Mesa Vulkan driver, moving to Windows expands Vulkan compatibility beyond official builds. The post outlines the challenges of porting to Win32, including driver interfaces, Windows graphics stack quirks, and stability considerations. It highlights the potential impact on gaming and open-source GPU tooling if such ports mature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mesa 的 RADV Vulkan 驱动走向 Windows，扩大了 Vulkan 的可用性，超出官方实现。文中概述了在 Win32 上移植时面临的挑战，如驱动接口、Windows 图形子系统的特性，以及稳定性权衡。若此类端口成熟，可能对游戏和开源 GPU 工具链产生深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.collabora.com/assets/images/blog/Collabora-Porting RADV.jpeg' data-full='https://www.collabora.com/assets/images/blog/Collabora-Porting RADV.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pranitha.dev/posts/tokio-gives-progress-not-ordering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tokio Gives Progress, Not Ordering: Scheduling 1M Tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tokio Gives Progress, Not Ordering: Scheduling 1M Tasks</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49070805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49070805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49070805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust’s Tokio runtime is examined for its approach to scheduling up to a million tasks by emphasizing progress updates over strict ordering, improving throughput and responsiveness of async applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解析 Rust 的 Tokio 运行时在调度百万级任务时，倾向通过进度更新而非严格排序来提升吞吐量和异步应用的响应性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49096742' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jcgt.org/published/0015/02/01/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Texture Lookup Approach to Bézier Curve Evaluation on the GPU (JCGT)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 GPU 上通过纹理查找实现贝塞尔曲线评估（JCGT）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49096742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49096742'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proposes using texture lookup to evaluate Bézier curves on the GPU, potentially enabling fast, memory-coherent curve evaluation for rendering and animation. This technique can trade CPU math with GPU texture fetch bandwidth, influencing rendering pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出在 GPU 上通过纹理查找实现贝塞尔曲线评估，可能在渲染与动画中实现更快、内存一致的曲线计算。这种技术在 CPU 计算与 GPU 纹理带宽之间权衡，影响着渲染流水线。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49090083' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49090083' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/cryptography-research-demo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic publishes a practical key-recovery attack on HAWK-256</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 发布对 HAWK-256 的实用密钥恢复攻击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49090083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49090083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49090083'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic released a demonstration showing a practical key-recovery attack against the HAWK-256 cryptosystem, highlighting non-trivial weaknesses in the scheme. The result is a reminder that cryptography must be carefully evaluated before deployment in security-critical contexts, especially in AI safety tooling where crypto is used for safeguards. The work likely includes a reproducible demo with steps and implications for protocol designers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 发布了对 HAWK-256 公钥或私钥的实用恢复攻击演示，暴露该密码体制的实际脆弱性。此结果提醒在安全场景中部署前需对密码学方案进行严格评估，尤其是在 AI 安全工具中对加密的依赖。相关工作包含可复现的演示与对系统设计者的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6d989fd0a6d2ece2be47e4891cf5c22de53d4682982cc7aafc4d924c2f8c47eb/anthropics/cryptography-research-demo' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6d989fd0a6d2ece2be47e4891cf5c22de53d4682982cc7aafc4d924c2f8c47eb/anthropics/cryptography-research-demo' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Idempotency keys enable safe retries for API requests by ensuring repeated submissions don’t duplicate effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>幂等性键通过确保重复提交不会导致重复副作用，从而实现对 API 请求的安全重试。文中探讨了如何实现和使用它们，以及这对 API 和客户端库的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨历史叙事如何走出希腊与罗马的框架，纳入非洲、亚洲、美洲等文明，重塑对古代科技、贸易与治理的理解。扩展视野有助于揭示全球交流网络的真实面貌，并影响当下的历史话语。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(480) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/research/revisiting-cpu-silent-data-corruptions-in-modern-datacenters/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A fresh look at CPU silent data corruption in modern data centers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Afresh look at CPU silent data corruption in modern data centers</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49147029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thorough look at the risk of silent data corruption in modern CPUs in data centers, detailing root causes, measurement highlights, and practical mitigations like ECC and architectural mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究聚焦现代数据中心 CPU 的静默数据损坏风险，剖析成因、度量结果与可行的缓解方法，如 ECC、RAS 等。提供对系统可靠性的实用建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49091895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49091895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49091895'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a lean approach to monitoring Spring Boot apps without heavy tooling, relying on built-in metrics, lightweight collectors, and simple dashboards. The technique reduces operational overhead while still delivering actionable visibility for production and edge deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供一种不依赖 Prometheus/Grafana 的轻量级 Spring Boot 监控方案，利用内置指标、轻量化采集和简易仪表盘实现可观测性。该方式降低运维开销，同时为生产端和边缘部署提供可用的数据洞察。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(485) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kamalfarahani/katharos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Katharos Functional programming and CSP-style concurrency for Python</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49143179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49143179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Katharos introduces CSP-style concurrency to Python, offering channels and safer primitives for building concurrent applications. It’s a notable approach for Python developers seeking safer, structured concurrency models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Katharos 将 CSP 风格的并发引入 Python，提供通道等安全原语，帮助构建更安全、结构化的并发应用。对于寻求更稳健并发模型的 Python 开发者具有参考意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trufflesecurity.com/blog/scanning-7-6-petabytes-of-ai-training-data-for-secrets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scanning 7.6 Petabytes of HuggingFace Training Data for Secrets</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49136955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49136955'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Truffle Security scanned 7.6 petabytes of public training data to search for secrets, uncovering exposed API keys and credentials. The finding underscores data provenance and secret-scanning practices in ML pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Truffle Security 对公开的训练数据进行 7.6PB 的扫描，发现暴露的密钥和凭据。这凸显了数据溯源和在机器学习管线中进行密钥扫描的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/cwida/ALP' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ALP: Adaptive lossless floating-point compression</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ALP：自适应无损浮点压缩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49051355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49051355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49051355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ALP is a library for adaptive lossless floating-point compression that aims to reduce memory bandwidth and storage without sacrificing precision.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ALP 是一个自适应无损浮点数据压缩库，目标是在不损失精度的前提下降低内存带宽与存储需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://waveformsecurity.com/blog/flume/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flume Water Monitor 915 MHz Security Is Pretty Good</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flume水监测器在915 MHz的安全性相当不错</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49105136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49105136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49105136'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security assessment of the Flume water monitor reveals robust wireless design and reasonable hardening for 915 MHz communications, with some caveats around device firmware update processes. The takeaway: IoT security is possible with careful protocol and firmware management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Flume 水监测系统的安全评估显示其 915 MHz 通信在设计与固件防护方面表现稳健，但固件更新流程仍有改进空间。结论是，通过合适的协议与更新机制，物联网安全可以做到可靠。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49104747' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49104747' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/funador/claude-code-merge-queue' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A local merge queue for parallel Claude Code agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用于并行 Claude Code 代理的本地合并队列</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49104747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49104747'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A local merge queue coordinates parallel Claude Code agents to work on the same codebase, speeding experimentation and reducing bottlenecks in AI-assisted development. It showcases practical orchestration tooling for multi-agent coding workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本地合并队列用于让多个 Claude Code 代理在同一代码库上并行工作，加速实验并减少 AI 辅助开发中的瓶颈。展示了多代理协作开发的落地工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49137419' data-tags='Science'>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fermi_paradox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fermi Paradox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>费米悖论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49137419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49137419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49137419'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Fermi Paradox questions why, given the high probability of extraterrestrial life in the universe, we see little evidence. Various explanations range from rare Earth scenarios to possible Great Filters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>费米悖论提出在宇宙中存在大量潜在文明的概率下，为何尚未观测到明确证据。讨论的解释从地球稀有到可能存在的伟大筛选等多种可能性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rjp.io/blog/2026-07-19-register-deprivation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Register deprivation: spills and runtime under forced register scarcity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>寄存器匮乏：被迫的寄存器稀缺导致的溢出与运行时成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49109019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49109019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece explains how forced register scarcity causes spilling, degrading performance in tight loops and numeric kernels. It discusses compiler strategies and potential hardware implications to mitigate the issue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解释了被迫的寄存器稀缺如何导致溢出，进而在密集运算中显著降低性能；讨论编译器的缓解策略及对硬件的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rjp.io/blog-assets/spillbench/fig1_runtime.png' data-full='https://rjp.io/blog-assets/spillbench/fig1_runtime.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uclahealth.org/news/release/drug-reverses-autism-like-brain-changes-adult-mice-within' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Drug reverses autism-like brain changes in adult mice within hours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>药物在成年小鼠中数小时内逆转自闭症样脑变化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49139052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49139052'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A drug reverses autism-like brain changes in adult mice within hours, offering a potential avenue for understanding neurodevelopmental plasticity. Human relevance remains uncertain, with the usual caveats about translating mouse findings to people.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种药物在成年小鼠体内数小时内逆转自闭症样脑变化，为理解神经可塑性提供潜在线索。与人类的应用还存在很大不确定性，需警惕将小鼠结果推及到人类的转化挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(499) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/culture/2026/07/30/how-to-spot-ai-writing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to Spot AI Writing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何识别 AI 写作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49134310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49134310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49134310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI writing detection remains imperfect even as tools proliferate. The Economist&#x27;s guide outlines practical cues and methodological caveats editors and educators can use to spot AI-authored text, from stylistic fingerprints to metadata signals. As AI capabilities grow, layered human-in-the-loop checks and disclosure norms become increasingly important.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管检测工具日益增多，AI 写作检测仍不完善。本文提供可操作的线索和方法论警戒，包括风格指纹与元数据等信号。随着 AI 能力提升，分层的人机协同审核与披露规范变得越来越重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49114527'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how trade secrets evolved from traditional know-how to modern IP strategies, weighing incentives for secrecy against openness in technology, and discussing regulatory and ethical implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>追溯贸易秘密的历史与未来，分析保密与开放在技术创新中的激励、法律与伦理影响，以及监管环境的演变。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49150181' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49150181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(501) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9851.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RFC 9851: TLS 1.2 is in Feature Freeze</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RFC 9851：TLS 1.2 已进入功能冻结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49150181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49150181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RFC 9851 marks TLS 1.2&#x27;s feature freeze, limiting changes to security fixes as the ecosystem stabilizes and TLS 1.3 gains ground. Operators should plan migrations accordingly while TLS 1.2 remains supported.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RFC 9851 标志 TLS 1.2 的功能冻结，未来仅允许修复安全漏洞等小改动，生态系统趋于稳定，TLS 1.3 的采用在推进。运营方应据此规划迁移路径，同时 TLS 1.2 仍得到支持。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49147890' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49147890' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(502) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.semenzin.com/let-the-machines-in/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let the machines in</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让机器进入</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147890' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49147890'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A perspective on embracing automation and AI agents in workflows, arguing that defensive guardrails should coexist with expanded machine decision-making. It touches on productivity gains and human-machine collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强调在工作流中让机器介入、使用自动化和AI 代理以提升生产力，同时讨论在安全边界与可控性之间的平衡。凸显人机协作的潜在收益。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49147853' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49147853' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(503) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://healeycodes.com/adding-defer-to-the-typescript-compiler' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Adding Go&#x27;s Defer to the TypeScript Compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 Go 的 defer 加进 TypeScript 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49147853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49147853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a compile-time feature proposal to bring Go&#x27;s defer semantics into TypeScript, enabling deterministic cleanup blocks without cluttering user code. It highlights potential benefits and the complexity of cross-language semantics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍将 Go 的 defer 语义带入 TypeScript 编译过程的设想，旨在实现更干净的资源清理与确定性执行。并讨论跨语言语义带来的复杂性与潜在收益。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49141562' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49141562' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(504) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebsite.pw/w/20260802-sizeof.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sizeof is surprisingly difficult to parse in C</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sizeof 在 C 语言中解析起来出乎意料的困难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49141562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49141562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49141562'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parsing sizeof in C reveals subtle complexities due to type categories, incomplete types, and compiler behaviors, complicating tooling that analyzes or transforms C code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C 语言中 sizeof 的解析因类型类别、 incomplete type、编译器行为等原因而变得复杂，给分析或转换 C 代码的工具带来挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(505) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mgaitan/pytest-leak-finder' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A tool for finding the causes of unstable Python tests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于发现不稳定 Python 测试原因的工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49113005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49113005'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tool for diagnosing unstable Python tests helps identify root causes of flakiness, using traces and reproducibility techniques to guide fixes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用于诊断不稳定 Python 测试的开源工具，帮助工程师通过追踪和复现性分析找出非确定性失败的根本原因，从而加速调试与 CI 流水线的可靠性提升。</p>
